 350) % 3) : b.text;
    const lines = wrap(c, text, BUBBLE_W - 28);
    const w = Math.min(BUBBLE_W, Math.max(64, ...lines.map((l) => Math.ceil(c.measureText(l).width))) + 28);
    return { lines, w, h: lines.length * LH + 20 };
  }
  function bubble(c, s, x, bottom) {
    c.font = FONT2(22);
    const { lines, w, h } = s;
    const box2 = pixelBox(c, x - w / 2, bottom - h - 4 * U, w, h, "#f4efe2", "#14110d");
    const tx = Math.round(x / U) * U;
    const ty = box2.y + box2.h - U;
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const half = (3 - i) * U;
      c.fillStyle = "#14110d";
      c.fillRect(tx - half - U, ty + i * U, (half + U) * 2, U);
      c.fillStyle = "#f4efe2";
      c.fillRect(tx - half, ty + i * U, half * 2, U);
    }
    c.fillStyle = "#14110d";
    c.fillRect(tx - U, ty + 3 * U, 2 * U, U);
    c.fillStyle = "#1b1712";
    lines.forEach((l, i) => c.fillText(l, x, box2.y + 10 + LH / 2 + i * LH + 1));
  }

  // src/game/action/audio/engine.ts
  var BUSES = ["master", "music", "sfx", "amb", "ui"];
  var DEFAULT_SETTINGS = { master: 0.8, music: 0.55, sfx: 0.9, amb: 0.7, ui: 0.6, muted: false };
  function cleanSettings(x) {
    const o = x && typeof x === "object" ? x : {};
    const out = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    for (const b of BUSES) {
      const v = Number(o[b]);
      if (Number.isFinite(v)) out[b] = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, v));
    }
    out.muted = o.muted === true;
    return out;
  }
  var sliderGain = (v) => v * v;
  var AudioScope = class {
    constructor(parent) {
      this.parent = parent;
      this.out = { ...parent, scope: this };
      parent.scope?.children.add(this);
    }
    out;
    nodes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    sources = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    children = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    sealed = false;
    disposed = false;
    node(node) {
      this.nodes.add(node);
      return node;
    }
    source(node) {
      this.node(node);
      this.sources.add(node);
      node.addEventListener("ended", () => {
        node.disconnect();
        this.sources.delete(node);
        this.nodes.delete(node);
        if (this.sealed && !this.sources.size) this.dispose();
      }, { once: true });
      return node;
    }
    seal() {
      this.sealed = true;
      if (!this.sources.size) this.dispose();
    }
    stopAt(at2) {
      const timer = this.source(this.out.ctx.createBufferSource());
      timer.buffer = this.out.ctx.createBuffer(1, 1, this.out.ctx.sampleRate);
      timer.addEventListener("ended", () => this.dispose(), { once: true });
      timer.start(Math.max(this.out.ctx.currentTime, at2));
    }
    dispose() {
      if (this.disposed) return;
      this.disposed = true;
      for (const child of this.children) child.dispose();
      for (const source of this.sources) try {
        source.stop();
      } catch {
      }
      for (const node of this.nodes) node.disconnect();
      this.nodes.clear();
      this.sources.clear();
      this.children.clear();
      this.parent.scope?.children.delete(this);
    }
  };
  var ownNode = (o, node) => o.scope?.node(node) ?? node;
  var ownSource = (o, node) => o.scope?.source(node) ?? node;
  function busOut(o, bus) {
    return { ...o, ...o.effects?.[bus] ?? {} };
  }
  function oneShot(o, render) {
    const scope = new AudioScope(o);
    try {
      return render(scope.out);
    } finally {
      scope.seal();
    }
  }
  function makeNoise(ctx, sec, brown = false) {
    const len = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * sec);
    const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
    const d3 = buf.getChannelData(0);
    let seed = brown ? 7 : 3;
    const rnd3 = () => (seed = seed * 1103515245 + 12345 >>> 0) / 4294967296 * 2 - 1;
    let last = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      const w = rnd3();
      if (brown) {
        last = (last + 0.02 * w) / 1.02;
        d3[i] = last * 3.5;
      } else d3[i] = w;
    }
    const fade = Math.min(2e3, len >> 3);
    for (let i = 0; i < fade; i++) {
      const a = i / fade;
      d3[len - fade + i] = d3[len - fade + i] * (1 - a) + d3[i] * a;
    }
    return buf;
  }
  function makeImpulse(ctx, sec, decay, dark2 = 0.5) {
    const len = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * sec);
    const buf = ctx.createBuffer(2, len, ctx.sampleRate);
    for (let c = 0; c < 2; c++) {
      const d3 = buf.getChannelData(c);
      let lp = 0;
      let seed = 17 + c * 31;
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        seed = seed * 1664525 + 1013904223 >>> 0;
        const w = seed / 2147483648 - 1;
        lp = lp + (w - lp) * (1 - dark2 * (i / len));
        d3[i] = lp * Math.pow(1 - i / len, decay);
      }
    }
    return buf;
  }
  var curves = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function driveCurve(k) {
    let c = curves.get(k);
    if (c) return c;
    c = new Float32Array(1024);
    for (let i = 0; i < 1024; i++) {
      const x = i / 1023 * 2 - 1;
      c[i] = (1 + k) * x / (1 + k * Math.abs(x));
    }
    curves.set(k, c);
    return c;
  }
  var mtof = (m) => 440 * Math.pow(2, (m - 69) / 12);
  function voice(o, dest, t, vol = 1, pan = 0, verb = 0, room2 = 0) {
    const ctx = o.ctx;
    const g = ownNode(o, ctx.createGain());
    g.gain.value = vol;
    let tail = g;
    if (pan) {
      const p = ownNode(o, ctx.createStereoPanner());
      p.pan.value = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, pan));
      g.connect(p);
      tail = p;
    }
    tail.connect(dest);
    if (verb > 0) {
      const s = ownNode(o, ctx.createGain());
      s.gain.value = verb;
      tail.connect(s).connect(o.verb);
    }
    if (room2 > 0) {
      const s = ownNode(o, ctx.createGain());
      s.gain.value = room2;
      tail.connect(s).connect(o.room);
    }
    void t;
    return g;
  }
  function perc(p, t, a, peak, d3) {
    p.cancelScheduledValues(t);
    p.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    p.linearRampToValueAtTime(peak, t + Math.max(1e-3, a));
    p.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + a + Math.max(5e-3, d3));
  }
  function adsr(p, t, a, d3, s, hold, r, peak = 1) {
    p.cancelScheduledValues(t);
    p.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    p.linearRampToValueAtTime(peak, t + Math.max(2e-3, a));
    p.setTargetAtTime(peak * s, t + a, Math.max(3e-3, d3 / 3));
    const end = t + Math.max(a + 5e-3, hold);
    p.setTargetAtTime(1e-4, end, Math.max(5e-3, r / 4));
    return end + r;
  }
  function osc(o, type, f, t, dur, dest) {
    const n = ownSource(o, o.ctx.createOscillator());
    n.type = type;
    n.frequency.setValueAtTime(f, t);
    n.connect(dest);
    n.start(t);
    n.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
    return n;
  }
  function noise(o, t, dur, dest, brown = false, rate = 1) {
    const n = ownSource(o, o.ctx.createBufferSource());
    n.buffer = brown ? o.brown : o.noise;
    n.loop = true;
    n.playbackRate.value = rate;
    n.connect(dest);
    n.start(t, Math.random() * 1.5);
    n.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
    return n;
  }
  function filt(o, type, f, q = 0.7, dest) {
    const n = ownNode(o, o.ctx.createBiquadFilter());
    n.type = type;
    n.frequency.value = f;
    n.Q.value = q;
    if (dest) n.connect(dest);
    return n;
  }
  function gainNode(o, v, dest) {
    const g = ownNode(o, o.ctx.createGain());
    g.gain.value = v;
    if (dest) g.connect(dest);
    return g;
  }
  function shaper(o, k, dest) {
    const n = ownNode(o, o.ctx.createWaveShaper());
    n.curve = driveCurve(k);
    n.oversample = "2x";
    if (dest) n.connect(dest);
    return n;
  }
  function buildGraph(ctx, dest) {
    const comp2 = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
    comp2.threshold.value = -10;
    comp2.knee.value = 8;
    comp2.ratio.value = 6;
    comp2.attack.value = 3e-3;
    comp2.release.value = 0.18;
    const master = ctx.createGain();
    master.connect(comp2).connect(dest);
    const bus = (v) => {
      const g = ctx.createGain();
      g.gain.value = v;
      g.connect(master);
      return g;
    };
    const music = bus(1);
    const sfx = bus(1);
    const amb = bus(1);
    const ui = bus(1);
    const duck = ctx.createGain();
    duck.connect(music);
    const longImpulse = makeImpulse(ctx, 2.6, 2.4, 0.7);
    const shortImpulse = makeImpulse(ctx, 0.45, 3, 0.3);
    const effects = {};
    for (const [name, target] of Object.entries({ music: duck, sfx, amb, ui })) {
      const verb = ctx.createConvolver();
      verb.buffer = longImpulse;
      const verbOut = ctx.createGain();
      verbOut.gain.value = 0.55;
      verb.connect(verbOut).connect(target);
      const room2 = ctx.createConvolver();
      room2.buffer = shortImpulse;
      const roomOut = ctx.createGain();
      roomOut.gain.value = 0.5;
      room2.connect(roomOut).connect(target);
      effects[name] = { verb, room: room2 };
    }
    const out = {
      ctx,
      sfx,
      ui,
      music: duck,
      amb,
      ...effects.sfx,
      effects,
      noise: makeNoise(ctx, 2),
      brown: makeNoise(ctx, 4, true)
    };
    return { out, gains: { master, music, sfx, amb, ui }, duck, comp: comp2 };
  }

  // src/game/action/audio/sfx.ts
  var rnd = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
  function thump(o, t, dest, f0, f1, dur, vol) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    perc(g.gain, t, 2e-3, vol, dur);
    const n = osc(o, "sine", f0, t, dur, g);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, f1), t + dur * 0.8);
  }
  function burst2(o, t, dest, type, f, q, dur, vol, a = 1e-3) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    perc(g.gain, t, a, vol, dur);
    const fl = filt(o, type, f, q, g);
    noise(o, t, dur + a, fl);
    return fl;
  }
  function tone2(o, t, dest, type, f, dur, vol, a = 3e-3) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    perc(g.gain, t, a, vol, dur);
    return osc(o, type, f, t, dur + a, g);
  }
  function bell(o, t, dest, f, dur, vol, ratio = 3.5, index = 3) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    perc(g.gain, t, 2e-3, vol, dur);
    const car = osc(o, "sine", f, t, dur, g);
    const mg = gainNode(o, 0);
    mg.gain.setValueAtTime(f * index, t);
    mg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f * 0.05, t + dur);
    mg.connect(car.frequency);
    osc(o, "sine", f * ratio, t, dur, mg);
  }
  function swoosh(o, t, dest, f0, f1, dur, vol) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + dur * 0.35);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    const fl = filt(o, "bandpass", f0, 1.4, g);
    fl.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur * 0.4);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f0 * 0.7, t + dur);
    noise(o, t, dur, fl);
  }
  function hit(level) {
    return (o, t, p) => {
      const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.08 * level, 0.25);
      const k = p.pitch * rnd(0.95, 1.05);
      const len = [0.1, 0.16, 0.28][level - 1];
      thump(o, t, v, [150, 130, 110][level - 1] * k, 40, len, [0.8, 0.95, 1.1][level - 1]);
      burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", [2600, 2e3, 1500][level - 1] * k, 1.2, [0.03, 0.045, 0.07][level - 1], [0.7, 0.8, 0.9][level - 1]);
      const body = gainNode(o, 0, v);
      perc(body.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.35 + level * 0.1, len * 0.7);
      const sh = shaper(o, 4 + level * 6, body);
      const b = osc(o, "triangle", 320 * k, t, len, sh);
      b.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(90 * k, t + len * 0.6);
      if (level === 3) {
        thump(o, t + 0.012, v, 70, 30, 0.35, 0.8);
        burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 900, 0.7, 0.22, 0.5);
      }
      return len + 0.4;
    };
  }
  var whiff = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 600 * p.pitch, 2400 * p.pitch, 0.16, 0.45);
    return 0.2;
  };
  var swingHeavy = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 300 * p.pitch, 1400 * p.pitch, 0.28, 0.6);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var kick = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.2);

    thump(o, t + 0.08, v, 140 * p.pitch, 45, 0.14, 0.9);
    burst2(o, t + 0.08, v, "bandpass", 1800, 1, 0.05, 0.7);
    return 0.4;
  };
  var lowKick = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0, 0.2);

    burst2(o, t + 0.05, v, "lowpass", 1500, 0.8, 0.08, 0.6);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var dashHit = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.15, 0.2);

    burst2(o, t + 0.12, v, "bandpass", 1300, 0.9, 0.09, 0.8);
    return 0.6;
  };
  var gun = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.25, 0.4);

  };
  var gunSilenced = (o, t, p) => {

  };
  var reload = (o, t, p) => {

  };

  var guard = (o, t, p) => {

  var guardBreak = (o, t, p) => {

    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(90, t + 0.4);
    thump(o, t, v, 90, 30, 0.3, 1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) tone2(o, t + 0.25 + i * 0.12, v, "square", 1500 - i * 200, 0.06, 0.06);

  };
  var parry = (o, t, p) => {

  };
  var evade = (o, t, p) => {

  var hurt = (o, t, p) => {

  var down = (o, t, p) => {

  var wakeup = (o, t, p) => {

  var ability = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.4, 0.3);

    fl.frequency.setValueAtTime(3e3, t);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(200, t + 0.7);
    const sh = shaper(o, 8, fl);
    noise(o, t, 0.75, sh, true, 2);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 300, 1800, 0.5, 0.35);
    return 1.2;
  };
  var superStart = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.6);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sawtooth", 110, 0.9, 0.25, 0.01);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, t + 0.6);
    const n2 = tone2(o, t, v, "sawtooth", 112, 0.9, 0.2, 0.01);
    n2.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(887, t + 0.6);
    burst2(o, t, v, "highpass", 6e3, 0.7, 0.5, 0.25, 0.3);
    bell(o, t + 0.55, v, 1760, 1.2, 0.35, 3.5, 4);
    burst2(o, t + 0.55, v, "bandpass", 3e3, 0.7, 0.15, 0.6);
    return 1.8;
  };
  var superHit = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5, 0.3);
    hit(3)(o, t, { ...p, dest: v, vol: 1, pan: 0 });
    thump(o, t + 0.02, v, 60, 25, 0.9, 1.1);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    perc(g.gain, t, 5e-3, 0.9, 1.1);
    const fl = filt(o, "lowpass", 2500, 0.7, g);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + 1.1);
    noise(o, t, 1.2, shaper(o, 12, fl), true, 3);
    return 1.6;
  };
  var heal = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.4);
    [72, 76, 79, 84].forEach((m, i) => bell(o, t + i * 0.07, v, mtof(m) * p.pitch, 0.5, 0.18, 2, 1.2));
    return 1;
  };
  var itemUse = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2500, 2, 0.06, 0.5);
    tone2(o, t + 0.08, v, "square", 660, 0.06, 0.12);
    tone2(o, t + 0.15, v, "square", 990, 0.1, 0.12);
    return 0.35;
  };
  var pickup = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.15);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 988 * p.pitch, 0.07, 0.15);
    tone2(o, t + 0.07, v, "square", 1319 * p.pitch, 0.15, 0.15);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var enemyDie = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.35);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    perc(g.gain, t, 0.02, 0.6, 0.8);
    const fl = filt(o, "bandpass", 3e3, 2, g);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(150, t + 0.8);
    noise(o, t, 0.85, fl);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "triangle", 880 * p.pitch, 0.7, 0.2, 0.01);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(110, t + 0.7);
    return 1;
  };
  var enemyTell = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.2);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sawtooth", 700 * p.pitch, 0.25, 0.18, 0.02);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1400 * p.pitch, t + 0.2);
    tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 1404 * p.pitch, 0.25, 0.12, 0.02);
    return 0.35;
  };
  var ghostWail = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    const dur = 1.3;
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.35, t + 0.3);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    const f = 420 * p.pitch;
    const a = osc(o, "sine", f, t, dur, g);
    const b = osc(o, "triangle", f * 1.5, t, dur, gainNode(o, 0.3, g));
    for (const n of [a, b]) {
      n.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(f * 1.25 * (n === b ? 1.5 : 1), t + 0.5);
      n.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(f * 0.8 * (n === b ? 1.5 : 1), t + dur);
    }
    const lg = gainNode(o, f * 0.03);
    lg.connect(a.frequency);
    osc(o, "sine", 6.5, t, dur, lg);
    const ng = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    ng.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    ng.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12, t + 0.3);
    ng.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    noise(o, t, dur, filt(o, "bandpass", f * 2, 3, ng));
    return dur + 0.5;
  };
  var ghostAttack = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 300, 2200, 0.25, 0.5);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sawtooth", 300, 0.3, 0.2, 0.02);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(900, t + 0.25);
    return 0.4;
  };
  var crawler = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.3);
    const n = 14;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const tt = t + i * rnd(0.03, 0.055);
      burst2(o, tt, v, "bandpass", rnd(2200, 4200) * p.pitch, 6, 0.012, rnd(0.25, 0.5));
    }
    return 0.9;
  };
  var stomp = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.15, 0.4);
    thump(o, t, v, 60 * p.pitch, 25, 0.35, 1.2);
    burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 400, 0.7, 0.25, 0.6);
    return 0.5;
  };
  var enemyShot = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.2);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 900, 1.2, 0.08, 0.7);
    thump(o, t, v, 150, 60, 0.08, 0.6);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var enemySpawn = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.6);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.5);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + 0.7);
    const fl = filt(o, "bandpass", 200, 3, g);
    fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(2500, t + 0.5);
    noise(o, t, 0.7, fl);
    tone2(o, t + 0.45, v, "sine", 55, 0.4, 0.5);
    return 1;
  };
  var whisper = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    const syll = 6 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);
    let tt = t;
    for (let i = 0; i < syll; i++) {
      const d3 = rnd(0.07, 0.16);
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
      perc(g.gain, tt, d3 * 0.3, rnd(0.15, 0.3), d3);
      const f1 = filt(o, "bandpass", rnd(500, 900), 8, g);
      const f2 = filt(o, "bandpass", rnd(1200, 2600), 8, g);
      const hs = filt(o, "highpass", 4e3, 0.7, gainNode(o, 0.4, g));
      const n = noise(o, tt, d3 * 1.3, f1);
      n.connect(f2);
      n.connect(hs);
      tt += d3 + rnd(0.01, 0.06);
    }
    return tt - t + 0.6;
  };
  var bossTell = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.4);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const tt = t + i * 0.16;
      tone2(o, tt, v, "square", 880, 0.1, 0.12);
      tone2(o, tt, v, "square", 932, 0.1, 0.1);
    }
    thump(o, t, v, 70, 35, 0.6, 0.8);
    return 0.8;
  };
  var bossRoar = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    const dur = 1.6;
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.9, t + 0.15);
    g.gain.setTargetAtTime(1e-4, t + dur * 0.6, 0.25);
    const sh = shaper(o, 30, g);
    const base2 = 70 * p.pitch;
    const a = osc(o, "sawtooth", base2, t, dur, sh);
    const b = osc(o, "sawtooth", base2 * 1.47, t, dur, sh);
    a.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(base2 * 0.75, t + dur);
    b.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(base2 * 1.1, t + dur);
    const am = gainNode(o, 0.35);
    am.connect(g.gain);
    osc(o, "sine", 23, t, dur, am);
    const ng = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    perc(ng.gain, t, 0.1, 0.4, dur);
    noise(o, t, dur, filt(o, "bandpass", 500, 1.5, ng), true, 4);
    return dur + 1;
  };
  var heartbeat = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    thump(o, t, v, 55, 35, 0.12, 0.9);
    thump(o, t + 0.22, v, 50, 30, 0.15, 0.7);
    return 0.5;
  };
  var siren = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol * 0.55, p.pan, 0.25);
    const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 2600, 0.8, v);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, lp);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.08);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.5, t + 1.35);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, t + 1.55);
    for (const [type, k] of [["sawtooth", 1], ["square", 1.5]]) {
      const n = osc(o, type, 620 * k, t, 1.55, g);
      n.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(980 * k, t + 0.75);
      n.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(620 * k, t + 1.5);
    }
    return 1.6;
  };
  var enemyStep = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3);
    thump(o, t, v, 75 * p.pitch, 40, 0.15, 0.7);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 500, 4, 0.1, 0.35);
    return 0.3;
  };
  function step(s) {
    return (o, t, p) => {
      const k = p.pitch * rnd(0.9, 1.1);
      const vol = p.vol * rnd(0.8, 1);
      switch (s) {
        case "marble": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.12, 0.3);
          burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 3200 * k, 2, 0.025, 0.5);
          burst2(o, t + 5e-3, v, "bandpass", 900 * k, 1.5, 0.05, 0.35);
          return 0.15;
        }
        case "concrete": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.03, 0.25);
          burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 1400 * k, 1, 0.04, 0.5);
          thump(o, t, v, 110 * k, 60, 0.05, 0.4);
          return 0.12;
        }
        case "carpet": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan);
          burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 500 * k, 0.7, 0.07, 0.55, 8e-3);
          return 0.12;
        }
        case "wood": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0, 0.3);
          burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 1100 * k, 2, 0.04, 0.45);
          tone2(o, t, v, "triangle", 190 * k, 0.07, 0.25);
          if (Math.random() < 0.15) {
            const n = tone2(o, t + 0.05, v, "sawtooth", 380, 0.18, 0.04, 0.05);
            n.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(330, t + 0.2);
          }
          return 0.3;
        }
        case "metal": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.4);
          burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2e3 * k, 1, 0.02, 0.4);
          tone2(o, t, v, "triangle", 517 * k, 0.18, 0.12);
          tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 1243 * k, 0.12, 0.08);
          tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 2071 * k, 0.08, 0.05);
          return 0.25;
        }
        case "tile": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.08, 0.35);
          burst2(o, t, v, "highpass", 3500 * k, 1, 0.02, 0.5);
          burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 1200 * k, 1.5, 0.04, 0.3);
          return 0.12;
        }
        case "wet": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.05, 0.2);
          const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
          perc(g.gain, t, 5e-3, 1.6, 0.12);
          const fl = filt(o, "bandpass", 600 * k, 5, g);
          fl.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1800 * k, t + 0.1);
          noise(o, t, 0.13, fl);
          return 0.2;
        }
        case "void": {
          const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, vol, p.pan, 0.4);
          thump(o, t, v, 60 * k, 45, 0.2, 0.5);
          const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 880 * k, 0.25, 0.05, 0.08);
          n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(840 * k, t + 0.25);
          return 0.4;
        }
      }
    };
  }
  var ding = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.35, 0.3);
    bell(o, t, v, 1318.5 * p.pitch, 2.2, 0.45, 1, 1.5);
    bell(o, t, v, 1318.5 * 2.76 * p.pitch, 1, 0.08, 1, 0.8);
    return 2.6;
  };
  var dingDong = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.35, 0.3);
    bell(o, t, v, 1318.5 * p.pitch, 1.4, 0.4, 1, 1.4);
    bell(o, t + 0.45, v, 1046.5 * p.pitch, 2, 0.4, 1, 1.4);
    return 2.6;
  };
  function doorSlide(open) {
    return (o, t, p) => {
      const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.3);
      const dur = 1.1;
      const mg = gainNode(o, 0, v);
      mg.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
      mg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.18, t + 0.15);
      mg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.18, t + dur - 0.2);
      mg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
      const m = osc(o, "sawtooth", 95, t, dur, filt(o, "lowpass", 300, 1, mg));
      m.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(open ? 110 : 85, t + dur);
      const rg = gainNode(o, 0, v);
      rg.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
      rg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.3, t + 0.2);
      rg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t + dur - 0.25);
      rg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
      noise(o, t, dur, filt(o, "bandpass", 700, 0.8, rg), true, 6);
      thump(o, t + dur - 0.05, v, open ? 120 : 90, 40, 0.12, open ? 0.4 : 0.9);
      burst2(o, t + dur - 0.05, v, "bandpass", 1500, 2, 0.04, open ? 0.2 : 0.5);
      return dur + 0.3;
    };
  }
  var panelButton = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0, 0.2);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 3500, 3, 0.012, 0.6);
    tone2(o, t + 0.01, v, "sine", 1975 * p.pitch, 0.09, 0.2);
    return 0.15;
  };
  var intercom = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    const dur = 0.9;
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.3, t + 0.03);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, t + dur - 0.15);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    const bp = filt(o, "bandpass", 1800, 1.2, g);
    noise(o, t, dur, shaper(o, 30, bp));
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) burst2(o, t + rnd(0, dur), v, "highpass", 2500, 1, 0.01, rnd(0.2, 0.5));
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1e3, 0.05, 0.08);
    return dur + 0.2;
  };
  var elevatorRide = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0, 0.3);
    const dur = 2.8;
    thump(o, t, v, 70, 35, 0.2, 0.8);
    burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 500, 0.7, 0.15, 0.4);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.6);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.5, t + dur - 0.6);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    const h = osc(o, "sawtooth", 48, t, dur, filt(o, "lowpass", 220, 2, g));
    h.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(52, t + dur * 0.5);
    h.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(46, t + dur);
    noise(o, t, dur, filt(o, "lowpass", 300, 0.7, gainNode(o, 0.6, g)), true);
    swoosh(o, t + 0.5, v, 200, 700, dur - 0.8, 0.15);
    thump(o, t + dur - 0.1, v, 80, 35, 0.15, 0.6);
    return dur + 0.3;
  };
  var ropeCreak = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3);
    const dur = rnd(0.5, 0.9);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.2, t + dur * 0.3);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    const f = rnd(18, 30);
    const s = osc(o, "sawtooth", f, t, dur, filt(o, "bandpass", rnd(600, 1100), 12, g));
    s.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(f * rnd(0.6, 1.4), t + dur);
    return dur + 0.4;
  };
  var uiMove = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1760 * p.pitch, 0.03, 0.14);
    return 0.06;
  };
  var uiOk = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.05);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1320 * p.pitch, 0.05, 0.1);
    tone2(o, t + 0.05, v, "square", 1980 * p.pitch, 0.09, 0.1);
    return 0.18;
  };
  var uiBack = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1320 * p.pitch, 0.05, 0.09);
    tone2(o, t + 0.05, v, "square", 880 * p.pitch, 0.08, 0.09);
    return 0.16;
  };
  var uiError = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 196, 0.09, 0.15);
    tone2(o, t + 0.11, v, "square", 185, 0.14, 0.15);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var uiOpen = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 1500, 5e3, 0.12, 0.15);
    tone2(o, t + 0.04, v, "triangle", 1046, 0.1, 0.12);
    return 0.2;
  };
  var uiClose = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    swoosh(o, t, v, 4e3, 1200, 0.1, 0.12);
    return 0.15;
  };
  var textBlip = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 600 * p.pitch * rnd(0.97, 1.03), 0.025, 0.05);
    return 0.04;
  };
  var message = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    bell(o, t, v, 1568, 0.25, 0.2, 2, 1);
    bell(o, t + 0.12, v, 2093, 0.4, 0.2, 2, 1);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    perc(g.gain, t, 0.01, 0.15, 0.25);
    osc(o, "square", 150, t, 0.25, filt(o, "lowpass", 300, 1, g));
    return 0.6;
  };
  var ruleStamp = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.25, 0.4);
    thump(o, t, v, 140, 50, 0.12, 1.1);
    burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 1800, 0.7, 0.09, 0.8);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 4e3, 1, 0.03, 0.4);
    [64, 68, 71, 76].forEach((m, i) => bell(o, t + 0.18 + i * 0.05, v, mtof(m), 0.9, 0.14, 1, 1.2));
    return 1.2;
  };
  var save = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.2);
    [67, 72, 79].forEach((m, i) => tone2(o, t + i * 0.08, v, "triangle", mtof(m), 0.25, 0.15));
    return 0.6;
  };
  var coin = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1975, 0.06, 0.12);
    tone2(o, t + 0.06, v, "square", 2637, 0.25, 0.12);
    return 0.35;
  };
  var levelUp = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3);
    [60, 64, 67, 72, 76, 79, 84].forEach((m, i) => tone2(o, t + i * 0.05, v, "square", mtof(m), 0.12, 0.08));
    bell(o, t + 0.36, v, mtof(84), 1, 0.25, 2, 2);
    return 1.4;
  };
  var doorWood = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.4);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.7, t + 0.1);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + 0.6);
    const s = osc(o, "sawtooth", 24, t, 0.6, filt(o, "bandpass", 1300, 14, g));
    s.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(14, t + 0.6);
    return 0.7;
  };
  var doorSlam = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.35, 0.4);
    thump(o, t, v, 95, 40, 0.25, 1.1);
    burst2(o, t, v, "lowpass", 1200, 0.7, 0.18, 0.7);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2500, 2, 0.04, 0.4);
    return 1;
  };
  var knock = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.15, 0.5);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const tt = t + i * 0.22 + (i === 2 ? 0.05 : 0);
      thump(o, tt, v, 160, 90, 0.06, 0.7);
      burst2(o, tt, v, "bandpass", 900, 3, 0.04, 0.5);
    }
    return 1;
  };
  var switchClick = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0, 0.3);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2800, 4, 0.015, 0.7);
    thump(o, t, v, 300, 150, 0.03, 0.3);
    return 0.1;
  };
  var doorLatch = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol * 0.7, p.pan, 0, 0.25);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2200 * p.pitch, 6, 0.012, 0.55);
    thump(o, t, v, 260, 120, 0.025, 0.25);
    burst2(o, t + 0.07, v, "bandpass", 1500 * p.pitch, 5, 0.018, 0.4);
    thump(o, t + 0.07, v, 180, 90, 0.04, 0.2);
    return 0.2;
  };
  var elevChime = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol * 0.45, p.pan, 0.25, 0.2);
    bell(o, t, v, 784 * p.pitch, 1.1, 0.3, 1, 0.6);
    return 1.3;
  };
  var crateBreak = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1, 0.3);
    thump(o, t, v, 120, 50, 0.15, 0.8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const tt = t + rnd(0, 0.25);
      burst2(o, tt, v, "bandpass", rnd(700, 2500), 3, rnd(0.02, 0.06), rnd(0.2, 0.5));
    }
    return 0.6;
  };
  var glassBreak = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3, 0.3);
    burst2(o, t, v, "highpass", 3e3, 0.7, 0.15, 0.7);
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const tt = t + rnd(0.02, 0.5);
      tone2(o, tt, v, "sine", rnd(3e3, 7e3), rnd(0.05, 0.2), rnd(0.05, 0.12));
    }
    return 0.9;
  };
  var flicker = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const tt = t + rnd(0, 0.5);
      const d3 = rnd(0.02, 0.08);
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
      perc(g.gain, tt, 2e-3, rnd(0.15, 0.3), d3);
      osc(o, "sawtooth", 120, tt, d3, filt(o, "highpass", 800, 1, g));
    }
    return 0.7;
  };
  var drip = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sine", rnd(1400, 2200) * p.pitch, 0.08, 0.25, 1e-3);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(n.frequency.value * 0.5 + 400, t + 0.06);
    return 0.6;
  };
  var wallGrind = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.2, 0.3);
    const dur = 1.5;
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.2);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.5, t + dur - 0.3);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + dur);
    noise(o, t, dur, shaper(o, 10, filt(o, "lowpass", 400, 4, g)), true, 8);
    osc(o, "sawtooth", 41, t, dur, filt(o, "lowpass", 160, 1, gainNode(o, 0.5, g)));
    return dur + 0.3;
  };
  var clockTick = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0, 0.3);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 3800 * p.pitch, 8, 0.012, 0.5);
    return 0.05;
  };
  var timerWarn = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.1);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 1046, 0.08, 0.12);
    tone2(o, t + 0.15, v, "square", 1046, 0.08, 0.12);
    return 0.3;
  };
  var noiseUp = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.4);
    const n = tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 300, 0.4, 0.25, 0.05);
    n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(600, t + 0.4);
    tone2(o, t, v, "sine", 317, 0.4, 0.15, 0.05);
    return 0.6;
  };
  var safeRoom = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    [67, 71, 74, 79].forEach((m, i) => bell(o, t + i * 0.12, v, mtof(m), 1.6, 0.16, 1, 1.4));
    return 2.2;
  };
  var scare = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.5);
    const dur = 1.2;
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    perc(g.gain, t, 5e-3, 0.7, dur);
    const sh = shaper(o, 25, g);
    [61, 62, 67, 68, 73].forEach((m) => osc(o, "sawtooth", mtof(m) * 0.5, t, dur, sh));
    burst2(o, t, v, "highpass", 2e3, 0.7, 0.8, 0.4);
    thump(o, t, v, 50, 25, 0.8, 1);
    return dur + 1;
  };
  var heels = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.25, 0.4);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const tt = t + i * 0.32;
      burst2(o, tt, v, "bandpass", 3600, 4, 0.02, 0.6);
      tone2(o, tt, v, "triangle", 1250, 0.03, 0.15);
    }
    return 1.6;
  };
  var duct = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.3, 0.5);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const tt = t + i * rnd(0.35, 0.5);
      thump(o, tt, v, 90, 50, 0.2, 0.6);
      tone2(o, tt, v, "triangle", rnd(300, 420), 0.4, 0.15);
      tone2(o, tt, v, "sine", rnd(900, 1300), 0.25, 0.06);
    }
    return 1.8;
  };
  var hatch = (o, t, p) => {
    const v = voice(o, p.dest, t, p.vol, p.pan, 0.2, 0.4);
    burst2(o, t, v, "bandpass", 2600, 5, 0.03, 0.7);
    tone2(o, t, v, "square", 820, 0.04, 0.1);
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, v);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t + 0.1);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, t + 0.2);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1e-4, t + 0.7);
    const s = osc(o, "sawtooth", 30, t + 0.1, 0.6, filt(o, "bandpass", 1500, 12, g));
    s.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(18, t + 0.7);
    thump(o, t + 0.7, v, 110, 45, 0.15, 0.7);
    return 1;
  };
  var d2 = (label2, group, fn2, bus = "sfx") => ({ label: label2, group, bus, fn: fn2 });

  var SFX_IDS = Object.keys(SFX);
  var SURFACE_SFX = {
    marble: "stepMarble",
    concrete: "stepConcrete",
    carpet: "stepCarpet",
    wood: "stepWood",
    metal: "stepMetal",
    tile: "stepTile",
    wet: "stepWet",
    void: "stepVoid"
  };

  // src/game/action/audio/music.ts
  var PC = { c: 0, d: 2, e: 4, f: 5, g: 7, a: 9, b: 11 };
  function noteToMidi(s) {
    const m = /^([a-gA-G])([#b]?)(-?\d)$/.exec(s);
    if (!m) throw new Error(`음 표기 오류: ${s}`);
    const acc = m[2] === "#" ? 1 : m[2] === "b" ? -1 : 0;
    return 12 * (Number(m[3]) + 1) + PC[m[1].toLowerCase()] + acc;
  }
  function parseNotes(src) {
    const toks = [];
    for (const raw of src.split(/\s+/)) {
      if (!raw || raw === "|") continue;
      const m = /^(.*)\*(\d+)$/.exec(raw);
      if (m) {
        const n = Number(m[2]);
        if (m[1] === "." || m[1] === "-") for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) toks.push(m[1]);
        else {
          toks.push(m[1]);
          for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) toks.push("-");
        }
      } else toks.push(raw);
    }
    const evs = [];
    let cur = null;
    toks.forEach((t, i) => {
      if (t === "-") {
        if (cur) cur.len++;
        return;
      }
      cur = null;
      if (t === ".") return;
      let vel = 0.8;
      let body = t;
      if (body.endsWith("!")) vel = 1, body = body.slice(0, -1);
      else if (body.endsWith("?")) vel = 0.5, body = body.slice(0, -1);
      cur = { step: i, notes: body.split("+").map(noteToMidi), len: 1, vel };
      evs.push(cur);
    });
    return { evs, steps: toks.length };
  }
  function parseDrums(src) {
    const s = src.replace(/[\s|]/g, "");
    const evs = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) if (s[i] !== ".") evs.push({ step: i, kind: s[i] });
    return { evs, steps: s.length };
  }
  var QUAL = {
    "": [0, 4, 7],
    m: [0, 3, 7],
    "7": [0, 4, 7, 10],
    maj7: [0, 4, 7, 11],
    m7: [0, 3, 7, 10],
    dim: [0, 3, 6],
    m7b5: [0, 3, 6, 10],
    sus4: [0, 5, 7],
    aug: [0, 4, 8],
    "5": [0, 7],
    add9: [0, 4, 7, 14],
    madd9: [0, 3, 7, 14]
  };
  function chord(name) {
    const m = /^([A-G])([#b]?)(.*)$/.exec(name);
    if (!m || !(m[3] in QUAL)) throw new Error(`화음 표기 오류: ${name}`);
    const root = PC[m[1].toLowerCase()] + (m[2] === "#" ? 1 : m[2] === "b" ? -1 : 0);
    const tones = QUAL[m[3]].map((iv) => {
      let n = 48 + (root + iv) % 12;
      while (n < 52) n += 12;
      if (iv >= 12) n += 12;
      return n;
    });
    tones.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    return { root: (root + 12) % 12, tones };
  }
  var NAMES = ["c", "c#", "d", "d#", "e", "f", "f#", "g", "g#", "a", "a#", "b"];
  var tok = (notes) => notes.map((n) => `${NAMES[n % 12]}${Math.floor(n / 12) - 1}`).join("+");
  function comp(chords, rhythm, shift = 0) {
    return chords.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map((c) => {
      const ns = chord(c).tones.map((n) => n + shift);
      return [...rhythm].map((ch) => ch === "x" ? tok(ns) : ch === "X" ? tok(ns) + "!" : ch).join(" ");
    }).join(" ");
  }
  function bass(chords, rhythm, oct = 2) {
    return chords.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map((c) => {
      const { root } = chord(c);
      const r = 12 * (oct + 1) + root;
      const pick3 = (ch) => ch === "r" ? r : ch === "o" ? r + 12 : ch === "f" ? r + 7 : ch === "b" ? r + 10 : -1;
      return [...rhythm].map((ch) => pick3(ch) >= 0 ? tok([pick3(ch)]) : ch).join(" ");
    }).join(" ");
  }
  function arp(chords, shape, shift = 12) {
    return chords.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map((c) => {
      const ts = chord(c).tones;
      return [...shape].map((ch) => {
        if (!/\d/.test(ch)) return ch;
        const i = Number(ch);
        return tok([ts[i % ts.length] + 12 * Math.floor(i / ts.length) + shift]);
      }).join(" ");
    }).join(" ");
  }
  function fm(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel, ratio, idx, idxDecay, a, d3, s, r) {
    const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
    const end = adsr(g.gain, t, a, d3, s, dur, r, vel);
    const car = osc(o, "sine", f, t, end - t, g);
    const mg = gainNode(o, 0);
    mg.gain.setValueAtTime(f * idx, t);
    mg.gain.setTargetAtTime(f * idx * 0.15, t, idxDecay);
    mg.connect(car.frequency);
    osc(o, "sine", f * ratio, t, end - t, mg);
  }
  var INST = {
    // 아케이드 리드: 네모파 + 톱니파, 늦게 들어오는 떨림
    lead(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.01, 0.12, 0.7, dur, 0.08, vel * 0.32);
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 3200, 1.2, g);
      const vib = gainNode(o, 0);
      vib.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
      vib.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(f * 0.012, t + Math.min(0.4, dur));
      const a = osc(o, "square", f, t, end - t, lp);
      const b = osc(o, "sawtooth", f * 1.004, t, end - t, gainNode(o, 0.6, lp));
      vib.connect(a.frequency);
      vib.connect(b.frequency);
      osc(o, "sine", 5.5, t, end - t, vib);
    },
    square(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 5e-3, 0.08, 0.5, dur, 0.05, vel * 0.25);
      osc(o, "square", f, t, end - t, filt(o, "lowpass", 2500, 0.7, g));
    },
    // 금관 찌르기: 필터가 확 열렸다 닫힌다
    brass(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.02, 0.2, 0.6, dur, 0.1, vel * 0.22);
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 400, 2, g);
      lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(400, t);
      lp.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(3500, t + 0.04);
      lp.frequency.setTargetAtTime(900, t + 0.05, 0.12);
      osc(o, "sawtooth", f, t, end - t, lp);
      osc(o, "sawtooth", f * 1.006, t, end - t, lp);
    },
    epiano(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      fm(o, dest, t, f, Math.min(dur, 1.6), vel * 0.28, 1, 1.8, 0.25, 3e-3, 0.9, 0.35, 0.3);
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      perc(g.gain, t, 1e-3, vel * 0.04, 0.15);
      osc(o, "sine", f * 14, t, 0.2, g);
    },
    fmbass(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      fm(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel * 0.55, 1, 2.5, 0.06, 2e-3, 0.25, 0.55, 0.05);
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      adsr(g.gain, t, 2e-3, 0.2, 0.7, dur, 0.05, vel * 0.3);
      osc(o, "sine", f, t, dur + 0.1, g);
    },
    bass(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 3e-3, 0.15, 0.6, dur, 0.06, vel * 0.45);
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 1400, 3, g);
      lp.frequency.setTargetAtTime(350, t, 0.06);
      osc(o, "sawtooth", f, t, end - t, lp);
    },
    pad(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.6, 0.5, 0.8, dur, 1.2, vel * 0.09);
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 1100, 0.7, g);
      for (const k of [0.995, 1, 1.006]) osc(o, "sawtooth", f * k, t, end - t, lp);
    },
    strings(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.12, 0.3, 0.85, dur, 0.4, vel * 0.13);
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 2600, 0.7, g);
      const vib = gainNode(o, f * 6e-3);
      for (const k of [0.997, 1.003]) vib.connect(osc(o, "sawtooth", f * k, t, end - t, lp).frequency);
      osc(o, "sine", 5, t, end - t, vib);
    },
    bell(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      fm(o, dest, t, f, 0.05, vel * 0.22, 3.5, 2.5, 0.4, 2e-3, 1.6, 1e-4, 1.2);
    },
    // 오르골: 사인 + 높은 배음, 짧게 사그라듦. 조금씩 음이 어긋난다
    box(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const ff = f * (1 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8e-3);
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      perc(g.gain, t, 2e-3, vel * 0.3, 1.4);
      osc(o, "sine", ff, t, 1.5, g);
      const h = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      perc(h.gain, t, 1e-3, vel * 0.08, 0.4);
      osc(o, "sine", ff * 5.02, t, 0.5, h);
      void dur;
    },
    organ(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.01, 0.1, 0.9, dur, 0.06, vel * 0.13);
      const vib = gainNode(o, f * 4e-3);
      for (const [k, v] of [[1, 1], [2, 0.5], [3, 0.25], [0.5, 0.4]]) {
        const n = osc(o, "sine", f * k, t, end - t, gainNode(o, v, g));
        vib.connect(n.frequency);
      }
      osc(o, "sine", 6, t, end - t, vib);
    },
    pluck(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      perc(g.gain, t, 2e-3, vel * 0.3, Math.min(0.5, dur + 0.15));
      const lp = filt(o, "lowpass", 3e3, 1, g);
      lp.frequency.setTargetAtTime(500, t, 0.08);
      osc(o, "triangle", f, t, 0.6, lp);
      osc(o, "square", f, t, 0.6, gainNode(o, 0.2, lp));
    },
    // 사람 목소리 같은 '아—' (모음 대역 둘)
    choir(o, dest, t, f, dur, vel) {
      const g = gainNode(o, 0, dest);
      const end = adsr(g.gain, t, 0.5, 0.4, 0.85, dur, 1, vel * 0.5);
      const f1 = filt(o, "bandpass", 700, 5, g);
      const f2 = filt(o, "bandpass", 1150, 6, g);
      for (const k of [0.996, 1.004]) {
        const s = osc(o, "sawtooth", f * k, t, end - t, f1);
        s.connect(f2);
      }
    }
  };
  function drum(o, dest, t, kind, vel) {
    const g = gainNode(o, vel, dest);
    switch (kind) {
      case "k": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.9, 0.25);
        const n = osc(o, "sine", 150, t, 0.3, e);
        n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(42, t + 0.12);
        const c = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(c.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.3, 0.02);
        noise(o, t, 0.03, filt(o, "lowpass", 3e3, 0.7, c));
        break;
      }
      case "s": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.45, 0.16);
        noise(o, t, 0.2, filt(o, "bandpass", 2200, 0.8, e));
        const b = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(b.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.35, 0.08);
        const n = osc(o, "triangle", 230, t, 0.1, b);
        n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(160, t + 0.08);
        break;
      }
      case "x": {
        for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
          const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
          perc(e.gain, t + i * 0.011, 1e-3, 0.4, i === 2 ? 0.12 : 0.01);
          noise(o, t + i * 0.011, 0.14, filt(o, "bandpass", 1300, 1.5, e));
        }
        break;
      }
      case "h":
      case "o": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, kind === "h" ? 0.18 : 0.15, kind === "h" ? 0.04 : 0.3);
        noise(o, t, kind === "h" ? 0.06 : 0.35, filt(o, "highpass", 7e3, 0.7, e));
        break;
      }
      case "c": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.22, 1.4);
        noise(o, t, 1.5, filt(o, "highpass", 4500, 0.5, e));
        break;
      }
      case "t":
      case "m": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.6, 0.3);
        const f0 = kind === "t" ? 110 : 170;
        const n = osc(o, "sine", f0, t, 0.35, e);
        n.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f0 * 0.6, t + 0.3);
        break;
      }
      case "r": {
        const e = gainNode(o, 0, g);
        perc(e.gain, t, 1e-3, 0.3, 0.03);
        osc(o, "triangle", 1700, t, 0.05, filt(o, "bandpass", 1700, 4, e));
        noise(o, t, 0.03, filt(o, "bandpass", 3e3, 3, e));
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  function compileSong(def) {
    const chans = {};
    for (const [name, c] of Object.entries(def.chans)) chans[name] = { def: c, at: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map() };
    let base2 = 0;
    for (const entry of def.order) {
      const m = /^([A-Za-z0-9_]+)([+-]\d+)?$/.exec(entry);
      if (!m || !def.pats[m[1]]) throw new Error(`곡 순서 오류: ${entry}`);
      const pat = def.pats[m[1]];
      const tr = Number(m[2] ?? 0);
      let len = 0;
      const parsed = {};
      for (const [name, src] of Object.entries(pat)) {
        const c = def.chans[name];
        if (!c) throw new Error(`없는 악기 줄: ${name}`);
        parsed[name] = c.inst === "drums" ? parseDrums(src) : parseNotes(src);
        len = Math.max(len, parsed[name].steps);
      }
      for (const [name, p] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
        for (const e of p.evs) {
          const ev = "notes" in e ? { ...e, notes: e.notes.map((n) => n + tr) } : e;
          const at2 = chans[name].at;
          const k = base2 + e.step;
          (at2.get(k) ?? at2.set(k, []).get(k)).push(ev);
        }
      }
      base2 += len;
    }
    return { def, steps: base2, chans };
  }
  var stepSec = (bpm) => 60 / bpm / 4;
  var Player2 = class {
    constructor(o, song, startAt) {
      this.o = o;
      this.song = song;
      this.startAt = startAt;
      this.scope = new AudioScope(busOut(o, "music"));
      o = this.o = this.scope.out;
      this.out = gainNode(o, 1, o.music);
      const verb = gainNode(o, 1, o.verb), room2 = gainNode(o, 1, o.room);
      this.sends = [this.out, verb, room2];
      this.o = { ...o, verb, room: room2 };
      this.nextTime = startAt;
      for (const [name, c] of Object.entries(song.chans)) {
        const d3 = c.def;
        const g = gainNode(o, d3.vol ?? 1);
        let tail = g;
        if (d3.drive) tail = tail.connect(shaper(o, d3.drive));
        if (d3.pan) {
          const p = ownNode(o, o.ctx.createStereoPanner());
          p.pan.value = d3.pan;
          tail = tail.connect(p);
        }
        tail.connect(this.out);
        if (d3.verb) tail.connect(gainNode(o, d3.verb, this.o.verb));
        this.chanNodes.set(name, g);
      }
    }
    out;
    scope;
    sends;
    chanNodes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    step = 0;
    nextTime;
    ended = false;
    /** until 시각까지의 칸을 미리 예약한다 */
    schedule(until, now = -Infinity) {
      const s = this.song;
      const sec = stepSec(s.def.bpm);
      while (!this.ended && this.nextTime < now - 0.05) {
        this.step++;
        this.nextTime += sec;
        if (!s.def.loop && this.step >= s.steps) this.ended = true;
      }
      while (!this.ended && this.nextTime < until) {
        const i = this.step % s.steps;
        const swing = i % 2 === 1 ? (s.def.swing ?? 0) * sec : 0;
        const t = this.nextTime + swing;
        for (const [name, c] of Object.entries(s.chans)) {
          const evs = c.at.get(i);
          if (!evs) continue;
          const dest = this.chanNodes.get(name);
          for (const e of evs) {
            if ("kind" in e) oneShot(this.o, (o) => drum(o, dest, t, e.kind, 1));
            else {
              const inst = INST[c.def.inst];
              for (const n of e.notes) {
                const sour = s.def.wrong && Math.random() < s.def.wrong ? (Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7) : 0;
                oneShot(this.o, (o) => inst(o, dest, t, mtof(n + sour), e.len * sec * 0.95, e.vel));
              }
            }
          }
        }
        this.step++;
        this.nextTime += sec;
        if (!s.def.loop && this.step >= s.steps) this.ended = true;
      }
    }
    /** 끝나는 데 걸리는 시간(초). 반복곡은 한 바퀴 */
    get lengthSec() {
      return this.song.steps * stepSec(this.song.def.bpm);
    }
    fadeOut(t, sec) {
      for (const node of this.sends) {
        const g = node.gain;
        g.cancelScheduledValues(t);
        g.setValueAtTime(g.value, t);
        g.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, t + sec);
      }
      this.ended = true;
      this.scope.stopAt(t + sec);
    }
  };

  var puzzle = {
    label: "퍼즐과 선택의 층",
