  var rnd2 = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
  function startAmbience(o, scene, fadeIn = 1.5) {
    const scope = new AudioScope(busOut(o, "amb"));
    o = scope.out;
    const ctx = o.ctx;
    const spec = AMBIENCE[scene];
    const out = gainNode(o, 0, o.amb);
    const verb = gainNode(o, 1, o.verb), room2 = gainNode(o, 1, o.room);
    o = { ...o, verb, room: room2 };
    const t0 = ctx.currentTime;
    out.gain.setValueAtTime(1e-4, t0);
    out.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(1, t0 + fadeIn);
    const long = 86400;
    for (const l of spec.layers) {
      const g = gainNode(o, l.vol, out);
      if (l.kind === "hum") {
        for (const [k, v] of [[1, 1], [2, 0.5], [3, 0.3], [4, 0.15]]) osc(o, "sine", l.f * k, t0, long, gainNode(o, v, g));
        const wob = gainNode(o, l.vol * 0.3);
        wob.connect(g.gain);
        osc(o, "sine", 0.13, t0, long, wob);
      } else if (l.kind === "rumble") {
        noise(o, t0, long, filt(o, "lowpass", l.f, 1.5, g), true);
        const wob = gainNode(o, l.vol * 0.4);
        wob.connect(g.gain);
        osc(o, "sine", 0.07, t0, long, wob);
      } else if (l.kind === "air") {
        noise(o, t0, long, filt(o, "bandpass", l.f, 0.6, g));
        const wob = gainNode(o, l.vol * 0.6);
        wob.connect(g.gain);
        osc(o, "sine", 0.05, t0, long, wob);
      } else {
        osc(o, "sine", l.f, t0, long, g);
      }
    }
    const timers = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const later = (f, ms) => {
      const id = window.setTimeout(() => {
        timers.delete(id);
        f();
      }, ms);
      timers.add(id);
    };
    let live = true;
    const fire = (e) => {
      if (!live) return;
      if (ctx.state !== "running") {
        later(() => fire(e), e.gap[0] * 1e3);
        return;
      }
      const vol = rnd2(e.vol[0], e.vol[1]);
      const pan = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (e.pan ?? 0);
      const pitch = e.pitch ? rnd2(e.pitch[0], e.pitch[1]) : 1;
      const t = ctx.currentTime + 0.05;
      if (e.id === "typing") {
        const n = 4 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10);
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) oneShot(o, (ao) => SFX.switchClick.fn(ao, t + i * rnd2(0.07, 0.16), { vol: vol * rnd2(0.5, 1), pan, pitch: rnd2(1.2, 1.8), dest: out }));
      } else oneShot(o, (ao) => SFX[e.id].fn(ao, t, { vol, pan, pitch, dest: out }));
      later(() => fire(e), rnd2(e.gap[0], e.gap[1]) * 1e3);
    };
    for (const e of spec.events) later(() => fire(e), rnd2(e.gap[0] * 0.3, e.gap[1] * 0.6) * 1e3);
    return {
      scene,
      stop(fade = 1.2) {
        if (!live) return;
        live = false;
        for (const id of timers) clearTimeout(id);
        timers.clear();
        const t = ctx.currentTime;
        for (const node of [out, verb, room2]) {
          node.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);
          node.gain.setValueAtTime(node.gain.value, t);
          node.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, t + fade);
        }
        scope.stopAt(t + fade);
      }
    };
  }

  // src/game/action/audio/index.ts
  var SCENE_SONG = {
    title: "title",
    hq: "hq",
    lobby: "lobby",
    chase: "chase",
    replica: "replica",
    trial: "trial",
    dark: "dark",
    silent: null,
    puzzle: "puzzle",
    service: "service",
    boss: "boss"
  };
  var SCENE_SURFACE = {
    title: "marble",
    hq: "wood",
    lobby: "marble",
    chase: "concrete",
    replica: "carpet",
    trial: "tile",
    dark: "wet",
    silent: "concrete",
    puzzle: "void",
    service: "metal",
    boss: "concrete"
  };
  var SCENES = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["title", "hq", "lobby", "chase", "replica", "trial", "dark", "silent", "puzzle", "service", "boss"]);
  var ALIAS3 = { doors: "doorOpen", rope: "ropeCreak", stamp: "ruleStamp", knockknock: "knock", static: "intercom" };
  function pickSfx(e, scene) {
    switch (e.type) {
      case "hit":
        if (e.kind === "kick") return "kick";
        if (e.kind === "low") return "lowKick";
        if (e.kind === "dash") return "dashHit";
        if (e.kind === "bullet") return "bulletHit";
        if (e.kind === "super") return "superHit";
        return e.strength && e.strength >= 3 ? "hit3" : e.strength === 2 ? "hit2" : "hit1";
      case "whiff":
        return e.strength && e.strength >= 3 ? "swingHeavy" : "whiff";
      case "shot":
        return e.kind === "silenced" ? "gunSilenced" : e.kind === "enemy" ? "enemyShot" : "gun";
      case "empty":
        return "emptyClick";
      case "guard":
        return e.perfect ? "parry" : "guard";
      case "super":
        return "superStart";
      case "item":
        return "itemUse";
      case "step":
        return SURFACE_SFX[e.surface ?? (scene ? SCENE_SURFACE[scene] : "concrete")];
      case "door":
        if (e.kind === "wood") return "doorWood";
        if (e.kind === "slam") return "doorSlam";
        return e.open === false ? "doorClose" : "doorOpen";
      case "ruleFound":
        return "ruleStamp";
      case "hp:low":
        return "heartbeat";
      case "custom":
        if (e.name && e.name in ALIAS3) return ALIAS3[e.name];
        return e.name && e.name in SFX ? e.name : null;
      default:
        return e.type in SFX ? e.type : null;
    }
  }
  var MIN_GAP = { textBlip: 0.03, uiMove: 0.03, heartbeat: 0.3 };
  var STEP_GAP = 0.07;
  var MAX_VOICES = 28;
  function createGameAudio(opts = {}) {
    let settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS };
    let ctx = null;
    let graph = null;
    let out = null;
    let disposed = false;
    let userPaused = false;
    let cutscene = false;
    const media = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    let scene = null;
    let amb = null;
    let song = null;
    let resumeSong = null;
    let jingleEnd = null;
    let rootScope = null;
    let pumpTimer = 0;
    const lastAt = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const voices = [];
    let listener = { x: 0, half: 640 };
    const compiled = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    const listeners = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    const ready = (async () => {
      try {
        const raw = await opts.load?.();
        if (raw != null && !disposed) settings = cleanSettings(typeof raw === "string" ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw);
      } catch {
      }
      applyVolumes();
    })();
    let saveTimer = 0;
    const saveNow = () => {
      saveTimer = 0;
      try {
        void Promise.resolve(opts.save?.(JSON.stringify(settings))).catch(() => {
        });
      } catch {
      }
    };
    const persist = () => {
      clearTimeout(saveTimer);
      saveTimer = window.setTimeout(saveNow, 400);
    };
    function syncMedia() {
      for (const track of media) track.setPolicy(settings.muted ? 0 : sliderGain(settings.master) * sliderGain(settings.sfx), userPaused);
    }
    function applyVolumes() {
      syncMedia();
      if (!graph || !ctx) return;
      const t = ctx.currentTime;
      for (const b of BUSES) {
        const v = b === "master" ? settings.muted || cutscene ? 0 : sliderGain(settings.master) : sliderGain(settings[b]);
        graph.gains[b].gain.cancelScheduledValues(t);
        if (v === 0) graph.gains[b].gain.setValueAtTime(0, t);
        else graph.gains[b].gain.setTargetAtTime(v, t, 0.03);
      }
    }
    let wantSong = null;
    function ensure() {
      return disposed ? null : out;
    }
    function create() {
      if (disposed) return null;
      if (out) return out;
      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return null;
      try {
        ctx = new AC({ latencyHint: "interactive" });
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
      graph = buildGraph(ctx, ctx.destination);
      rootScope = new AudioScope(graph.out);
      out = rootScope.out;
      applyVolumes();
      ctx.addEventListener("statechange", () => {
        if (disposed) return;
        if (ctx?.state !== "running" && opts.autoUnlock !== false) attachUnlock();
        listeners.forEach((f) => f());
      });
      return out;
    }
    async function unlock() {
      const fresh = !out;
      const o = create();
      if (!o || !ctx) return false;
      const current = ctx;
      if (fresh) {
        if (scene) amb = startAmbience(o, scene);
        music(wantSong, 1.2);
      }
      if (userPaused || document.hidden) return false;
      try {
        const nav = navigator;
        if (nav.audioSession && nav.audioSession.type !== "playback") nav.audioSession.type = "playback";
      } catch {
      }
      if (current.state !== "running") {
        const prime = new AudioScope(o);
        try {
          const b = prime.source(current.createBufferSource());
          b.buffer = current.createBuffer(1, 1, 22050);
          b.connect(current.destination);
          b.start(0);
          prime.seal();
          await current.resume();
        } catch {
          prime.dispose();
          return false;
        }
      }
      if (disposed || ctx !== current) return false;
      if (userPaused || document.hidden) {
        void current.suspend().catch(() => {
        });
        return false;
      }
      if (current.state === "running") {
        detachUnlock();
        pump();
      }
      return current.state === "running";
    }
    const UNLOCK_EVENTS = ["pointerdown", "touchend", "mousedown", "keydown"];
    const onGesture = () => void unlock();
    function attachUnlock() {
      for (const ev of UNLOCK_EVENTS) window.addEventListener(ev, onGesture, { capture: true, passive: true });
    }
    function detachUnlock() {
      for (const ev of UNLOCK_EVENTS) window.removeEventListener(ev, onGesture, { capture: true });
    }
    if (opts.autoUnlock !== false) attachUnlock();
    const onVis = () => {
      if (!ctx) return;
      if (document.hidden) void ctx.suspend().catch(() => {
      });
      else if (!userPaused) void unlock();
    };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    function play(id, o = {}) {
      const ao = ensure();
      if (!ao || !ctx || ctx.state !== "running" || userPaused || document.hidden) return false;
      const def = SFX[id];
      if (!def || cutscene) return false;
      const now = ctx.currentTime;
      const gap = def.group === "step" ? STEP_GAP : MIN_GAP[id] ?? 0.015;
      if (now - (lastAt.get(id) ?? -1) < gap) return false;
      while (voices.length && voices[0] < now) voices.shift();
      if (voices.length >= MAX_VOICES && (def.group === "step" || def.group === "world")) return false;
      lastAt.set(id, now);
      const t = Math.max(now, o.at ?? now) + 5e-3;
      const dur = oneShot(busOut(ao, def.bus), (scoped) => def.fn(scoped, t, { vol: o.vol ?? 1, pan: o.pan ?? 0, pitch: o.pitch ?? 1, dest: ao[def.bus] }));
      const end = t + dur;
      const i = voices.findIndex((v) => v > end);
      voices.splice(i < 0 ? voices.length : i, 0, end);
      return true;
    }
    function panOf(e) {
      if (e.pan != null) return e.pan;
      if (e.x == null) return 0;
      return Math.max(-0.8, Math.min(0.8, (e.x - listener.x) / listener.half));
    }
    function event(e) {
      switch (e.type) {
        case "enter:floor":
          if (e.floor?.type && SCENES.has(e.floor.type)) floor(e.floor.type);
          return true;
        case "combat:boss":
          music("boss", 0.6);
          return true;
        case "combat:clear":
          if (song?.id === "boss") jingle("clear", scene ? SCENE_SONG[scene] : null);
          return true;
        case "run:end":
          jingle(e.how === "evac" || e.how === "death" ? "gameover" : "clear", null);
          return true;
      }
      const id = pickSfx(e, scene);
      if (!id) return false;
      const far = e.x == null ? 1 : Math.max(0.35, 1 - Math.max(0, Math.abs(e.x - listener.x) - listener.half) / (listener.half * 2));
      const ok = play(id, { vol: (e.vol ?? 1) * far, pan: panOf(e), pitch: e.pitch });
      if (ok && e.type === "super") duck(1.6);
      if (ok && e.type === "ruleFound") duck(1.2, 0.4);
      return ok;
    }
    function songOf(id) {
      let c = compiled.get(id);
      if (!c) compiled.set(id, c = compileSong(SONGS[id]));
      return c;
    }
    function pump() {
      if (!ctx || !song || ctx.state !== "running" || userPaused || document.hidden) return;
      if (jingleEnd !== null && ctx.currentTime >= jingleEnd) {
        music(resumeSong, 0.5);
        return;
      }
      song.player.schedule(ctx.currentTime + 0.4, ctx.currentTime);
    }
    function startPump() {
      if (!pumpTimer) pumpTimer = window.setInterval(pump, 50);
    }
    function music(id, fade = 1) {
      wantSong = id;
      jingleEnd = null;
      resumeSong = null;
      const o = ensure();
      if (!o || !ctx) return;
      if (song?.id === id && !song.player.ended) return;
      const t = ctx.currentTime;
      song?.player.fadeOut(t, fade);
      song = null;
      if (!id) {
        clearInterval(pumpTimer);
        pumpTimer = 0;
        return;
      }
      const player = new Player2(o, songOf(id), t + Math.min(0.15, fade * 0.3));
      song = { id, player };
      startPump();
      pump();
    }
    function jingle(id, then) {
      const o = ensure();
      if (!o || !ctx) return;
      const back = then !== void 0 ? then : resumeSong ?? song?.id ?? null;
      music(id, 0.25);
      resumeSong = back;
      jingleEnd = song.player.startAt + song.player.lengthSec + 0.8;
    }
    function duck(sec, to = 0.25) {
      if (!ctx || !graph) return;
      const g = graph.duck.gain;
      const t = ctx.currentTime;
      g.cancelScheduledValues(t);
      g.setTargetAtTime(to, t, 0.04);
      g.setTargetAtTime(1, t + sec, 0.3);
    }
    function floor(s) {
      if (s === scene) return;
      scene = s;
      wantSong = s ? SCENE_SONG[s] : null;
      const o = ensure();
      if (!o) return;
      amb?.stop();
      amb = s ? startAmbience(o, s) : null;
      music(s ? SCENE_SONG[s] : null, 1.2);
    }
    function setPaused(paused) {
      if (disposed || userPaused === paused) return;
      userPaused = paused;
      syncMedia();
      if (paused) void ctx?.suspend().catch(() => {
      });
      else if (ctx) void unlock();
    }
    return {
      ready,
      unlock,
      /** Called once for each story media element, before its first play(). */
      attachMedia(track) {
        if (disposed) return track.dispose();
        media.add(track);
        track.onDispose(() => media.delete(track));
        syncMedia();
      },
      setCutscene(active) {
        if (cutscene === active) return;
        cutscene = active;
        applyVolumes();
      },
      setPaused(paused) {
        setPaused(paused);
      },
      get unlocked() {
        return ctx?.state === "running";
      },
      get scene() {
        return scene;
      },
      get song() {
        return song?.id ?? null;
      },
      play,
      event,
      music,
      jingle,
      duck,
      floor,
      /** 소리의 좌우를 정할 기준: 화면 가운데 x와 화면 반 너비 */
      setListener(x, half = 640) {
        listener = { x, half: Math.max(1, half) };
      },
      get settings() {
        return { ...settings };
      },
      setVolume(bus, v) {
        settings = cleanSettings({ ...settings, [bus]: v });
        applyVolumes();
        persist();
      },
      setMuted(m) {
        settings = { ...settings, muted: m };
        applyVolumes();
        persist();
      },
      /** 게임 일시정지: 모든 소리를 멈춘다 */
      pause() {
        setPaused(true);
      },
      resume() {
        setPaused(false);
      },
      onState(f) {
        listeners.add(f);
        return () => listeners.delete(f);
      },
      dispose() {
        if (disposed) return;
        disposed = true;
        detachUnlock();
        document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
        clearInterval(pumpTimer);
        clearTimeout(saveTimer);
        if (saveTimer) saveNow();
        jingleEnd = null;
        amb?.stop(0);
        rootScope?.dispose();
        listeners.clear();
        for (const track of media) track.dispose();
        media.clear();
        song = null;
        amb = null;
        scene = null;
        void ctx?.close().catch(() => {
        });
        ctx = null;
        out = null;
        graph = null;
      }
    };
  }

  // src/game/action/audio/videoTrack.ts
  var VIDEO_AUDIO_EVENT = "goei:video-audio";
  var VideoTrack = class {
    constructor(root, url, blockedButton) {
      this.blockedButton = blockedButton;
      this.element = new Audio(url);
      document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", this.visibility);
      root.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(VIDEO_AUDIO_EVENT, { bubbles: true, detail: this }));
      this.sync();
    }
    element;
    disposed = false;
    held = false;
    gamePaused = false;
    pending = false;
    resync = false;
    needsGesture = false;
    cleanup = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    visibility = () => this.sync();
    get blocked() {
      return this.gamePaused || document.hidden;
    }
    setPolicy(volume, paused) {
      this.element.volume = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume));
      const changed = this.gamePaused !== paused;
      this.gamePaused = paused;
      if (changed) this.sync();
    }
    onDispose(fn2) {
      this.cleanup.add(fn2);
    }
    pause() {
      this.held = true;
      this.sync();
    }
    resume(at2) {
      this.element.currentTime = at2;
      this.held = false;
      this.sync();
    }
    enable(at2) {
      if (at2 !== void 0) this.element.currentTime = at2;
      this.needsGesture = false;
      this.sync();
    }
    sync() {
      const a = this.element;
      if (this.disposed || this.held || this.blocked) {
        this.resync = false;
        a.pause();
        return;
      }
      if (this.pending) {
        this.resync = true;
        return;
      }
      if (!a.paused || this.needsGesture) return;
      this.pending = true;
      void a.play().then(() => {
        if (this.disposed || this.held || this.blocked) a.pause();
        else this.blockedButton(false);
      }).catch((e) => {
        if (this.disposed || this.held || this.blocked) return;
        this.needsGesture = e instanceof DOMException && e.name === "NotAllowedError";
        this.blockedButton(this.needsGesture);
      }).finally(() => {
        this.pending = false;
        const retry = this.resync;
        this.resync = false;
        if (retry) this.sync();
      });
    }
    dispose() {
      if (this.disposed) return;
      this.disposed = true;
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", this.visibility);
      this.element.pause();
      this.element.removeAttribute("src");
      this.element.load();
      for (const fn2 of this.cleanup) fn2();
      this.cleanup.clear();
    }
  };

  // src/game/action/ui/fx.ts
  var W3 = W;
  var H3 = H;
  var size = (c) => (W3 = c.canvas.width, H3 = c.canvas.height);
  function off(w, h) {
    const cv = typeof OffscreenCanvas !== "undefined" ? new OffscreenCanvas(w, h) : Object.assign(document.createElement("canvas"), { width: w, height: h });
    return cv.getContext("2d");
  }
  function fit(c, w, h) {
    const cv = c.canvas;
    if (cv.width < w || cv.height < h) cv.width = Math.max(cv.width, Math.ceil(w)), cv.height = Math.max(cv.height, Math.ceil(h));
    c.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    c.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
    c.globalAlpha = 1;
    c.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
  }
  var Fx = class {
    opt = { shake: 1, flash: true, numbers: true };
    shakeT = 0;
    shakeDur = 0;
    shakePow = 0;
    flashes = [];
    sparks = [];
    rings = [];
    pops = [];
    title = null;
    dim = 0;
    tr = null;
    time = 0;
    m1 = off(256, 256);
    m2 = off(256, 256);
    dark = off(1800, 720);
    /** 진동 같은 바깥 반응 (게임패드·폰) */
    rumble = null;
    onEvent(e) {
      switch (e.type) {
        case "hit": {
          const s = e.strength;
          this.shake(3 + s * 4, 0.1 + s * 0.06);
          this.burst(e.x, e.y, 10 + s * 7, e.target === "player" ? "#ff6050" : "#ffe0a0", 3 + s * 1.5);
          this.ring(e.x, e.y, 16, 60 + s * 45, s === 3 ? "#fff" : "#ffd890", 4 + s * 3, 0.2 + s * 0.05);
          if (e.dmg != null && Math.round(e.dmg) > 0 && this.opt.numbers) this.pop(e.x, e.y - 30, String(Math.round(e.dmg)), e.target === "player" ? "#ff7060" : s === 3 ? "#ffe070" : "#fff", s >= 2);
          if (s === 3) this.flash("#fff", 0.08, 0.35);
          this.rumble?.(0.2 + s * 0.2, 60 + s * 30);
          break;
        }
        case "guard":
          this.burst(e.x, e.y, 8, "#9fd8ff", 3);
          this.ring(e.x, e.y, 20, 60, "#9fd8ff", 4, 0.2);
          this.shake(2, 0.06);
          break;
        case "down":
          this.shake(6, 0.15);
          this.burst(e.x, e.y, 10, "#b0a490", 3, -1);
          break;
        case "hurt":
          this.shake(5 + (e.strength ?? 1) * 3, 0.18);
          this.flash("#ff2020", 0.1, 0.2);
          this.rumble?.(0.6, 140);
          break;
        case "blast":
          this.shake(10, 0.3);
          this.ring(e.x, e.y, 20, e.r ?? 220, "#ffb070", 8, 0.35);
          this.burst(e.x, e.y, 26, "#ffb070", 5);
          this.flash("#ffd8a0", 0.12, 0.3);
          this.rumble?.(1, 250);
          break;
        case "ult":
          this.dim = 0.7;
          this.flash("#fff", 0.15, 0.5);
          this.rumble?.(0.8, 200);
          break;
        case "heal":
          this.burst(e.x, e.y, 12, "#80f0a0", 2, -1.5);
          if (e.amount && this.opt.numbers) this.pop(e.x, e.y - 40, `+${e.amount}`, "#80f0a0", false);
          break;
        case "pop":
          this.pop(e.x, e.y, e.text, e.color ?? "#fff", false);
          break;
        case "floor":
          this.title = { depth: e.depth, name: e.name, variant: e.variant, t: 0 };
          break;
      }
    }
    /** 엔진 GameEvent(engine/contract.ts)를 그대로 받는다. y 는 화면 y(발 위치 − 높이), 없으면 opt.y */
    onGameEvent(e, opt = {}) {
      const n = (k, d3 = 0) => typeof e[k] === "number" ? e[k] : d3;
      const x = n("x"), y = typeof e.y === "number" ? n("y") - n("z") : opt.y ?? 520;
      switch (e.type) {
        case "hit":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "hit", x, y, strength: Math.max(1, Math.min(3, n("strength", 1))), dmg: n("dmg"), target: e.target === "player" ? "player" : "enemy" });
        case "guard":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "guard", x, y });
        case "guardBreak":
          this.shake(8, 0.2);
          this.ring(x, y, 20, 120, "#9fd8ff", 6, 0.3);
          return this.flash("#9fd8ff", 0.12, 0.3);
        case "hurt":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "hurt", strength: n("dmg") > 20 ? 2 : 1 });
        case "down":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "down", x, y: opt.y ?? 600 });
        case "super":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "ult" });
        case "ability":
          return this.shake(4, 0.1);
        case "heal":
          return this.onEvent({ type: "heal", x, y });
        case "bossRoar":
          return this.shake(10, 0.6);
        case "ruleFound":
          return this.flash("#f0d890", 0.25, 0.18);
        case "enter:floor": {
          const f = e.floor ?? {};
          return this.onEvent({ type: "floor", depth: f.depth == null ? "" : f.depth <= 3 ? `L${f.depth}` : `B-${f.depth - 3}`, name: f.title ?? "" });
        }
      }
    }
    shake(pow, dur) {
      if (pow * this.opt.shake < this.shakePow * (this.shakeT / (this.shakeDur || 1))) return;
      this.shakePow = pow * this.opt.shake;
      this.shakeT = this.shakeDur = dur;
    }
    flash(color, dur, a = 0.4) {
      if (!this.opt.flash) return;
      this.flashes.push({ color, t: 0, dur, a });
    }
    burst(x, y, n, color, speed, lift = 0) {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, v = (0.4 + Math.random()) * speed * 60;
        this.sparks.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * v, vy: Math.sin(a) * v * 0.7 + lift * 60, t: 0, life: 0.18 + Math.random() * 0.22, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 3 });
      }
    }
    ring(x, y, r0, r1, color, width, life) {
      this.rings.push({ x, y, r0, r1, color, width, life, t: 0 });
    }
    pop(x, y, text, color, big) {
      let n = 0;
      for (const p of this.pops) if (p.t < 0.6 && Math.abs(p.x - x) < 70 && Math.abs(p.y - y) < 90) n++;
      const side = n % 2 ? 1 : -1;
      this.pops.push({ x: x + (n ? side * (28 + 14 * n) : (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16), y: y - n * 34, text, color, t: 0, big });
    }
    /** 흔들림 오프셋: 카메라에 더한다 */
    camera() {
      if (this.shakeT <= 0) return { x: 0, y: 0 };
      const k = this.shakeT / this.shakeDur;
      const p = this.shakePow * k;
      return { x: Math.round((Math.random() * 2 - 1) * p), y: Math.round((Math.random() * 2 - 1) * p * 0.6) };
    }
    get busy() {
      return !!this.tr;
    }
    update(dt) {
      this.time += dt;
      this.shakeT = Math.max(0, this.shakeT - dt);
      for (const f of this.flashes) f.t += dt;
      this.flashes = this.flashes.filter((f) => f.t < f.dur);
      for (const s of this.sparks) s.t += dt, s.x += s.vx * dt, s.y += s.vy * dt, s.vy += 900 * dt, s.vx *= 0.9;
      this.sparks = this.sparks.filter((s) => s.t < s.life);
      for (const r of this.rings) r.t += dt;
      this.rings = this.rings.filter((r) => r.t < r.life);
      for (const p of this.pops) p.t += dt, p.y -= dt * (p.t < 0.2 ? 160 : 30);
      this.pops = this.pops.filter((p) => p.t < 0.9);
      if (this.title) {
        this.title.t += dt;
        if (this.title.t > 1.9) this.title = null;
      }
      this.dim = Math.max(0, this.dim - dt * 1.2);
      if (this.tr) {
        const tr = this.tr;
        tr.t += dt;
        if (!tr.midDone && tr.t >= tr.dur / 2) {
          tr.midDone = true;
          tr.mid?.();
        }
        if (tr.t >= tr.dur) {
          this.tr = null;
          tr.done();
        }
      }
    }
    /** 월드 좌표 효과 (카메라 변환을 걸고 부른다) */
    drawWorld(c) {
      c.save();
      for (const r of this.rings) {
        const k = r.t / r.life;
        c.globalAlpha = 1 - k;
        c.strokeStyle = r.color;
        c.lineWidth = r.width * (1 - k * 0.6);
        c.beginPath();
        c.ellipse(r.x, r.y, r.r0 + (r.r1 - r.r0) * easeOut(k), (r.r0 + (r.r1 - r.r0) * easeOut(k)) * 0.75, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        c.stroke();
      }
      c.globalAlpha = 1;
      c.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
      for (const s of this.sparks) {
        c.globalAlpha = 1 - s.t / s.life;
        c.fillStyle = s.color;
        c.fillRect(s.x - s.size / 2, s.y - s.size / 2, s.size, s.size);
        c.fillRect(s.x - s.vx * 0.02, s.y - s.vy * 0.02, s.size * 0.6, s.size * 0.6);
      }
      c.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
      for (const p of this.pops) {
        const a = p.t > 0.6 ? 1 - (p.t - 0.6) / 0.3 : 1;
        const sc = p.t < 0.1 ? 1 + (0.1 - p.t) * 6 : 1;
        c.globalAlpha = a;
        setPixelText(true);
        label(c, p.text, p.x, p.y, (p.big ? 46 : 34) * sc, p.color, "center", 900);
        setPixelText(false);
      }
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 화면 덮개: 필살 암전·섬광·체력 위험 테두리·층 제목. HUD 보다 먼저 */
    drawScreen(c, hpRatio = 1) {
      size(c);
      c.save();
      c.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      if (this.dim > 0) c.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${Math.min(0.6, this.dim)})`, c.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
      if (hpRatio < 0.3) {
        const k = (0.3 - hpRatio) / 0.3;
        const pulse = 0.55 + Math.sin(this.time * (4 + k * 4)) * 0.25;
        const g = c.createRadialGradient(W3 / 2, H3 / 2, H3 * 0.35, W3 / 2, H3 / 2, H3 * 0.95);
        g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(120,0,0,0)");
        g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(150,0,0,${0.55 * k * pulse + 0.1})`);
        c.fillStyle = g;
        c.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
      }
      for (const f of this.flashes) {
        c.globalAlpha = f.a * (1 - f.t / f.dur);
        c.fillStyle = f.color;
        c.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
      }
      c.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (this.title) this.drawTitle(c, this.title);
      c.restore();
    }
    drawTitle(c, t) {
      const inK = Math.min(1, t.t / 0.3), outK = t.t > 1.5 ? (t.t - 1.5) / 0.4 : 0;
      c.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, inK * (1 - outK));
      const text = t.name.replace(/^\s*(L\d+|B-?\d+)\s*·\s*/, "") || t.depth;
      c.font = pfont2(22).f;
      const pw = Math.round(c.measureText(text).width + 40) + 3 & ~3, ph = t.variant ? 64 : 44, px = Math.round(W3 - 16 - pw) >> 2 << 2, py = 62;
      c.fillStyle = "#0c0f12";
      c.fillRect(px - 4, py - 4, pw + 8, ph + 8);
      c.fillStyle = "#b8873a";
      c.fillRect(px, py, pw, ph);
      c.fillStyle = "rgba(16,20,24,0.94)";
      c.fillRect(px + 4, py + 4, pw - 8, ph - 8);
      c.textAlign = "center";
      c.textBaseline = "middle";
      c.fillStyle = "#ece2c8";
      c.fillText(text, px + pw / 2, py + 22);
      if (t.variant) c.font = pfont2(11).f, c.fillStyle = "#f0b45a", c.fillText(`변형 · ${t.variant}`, px + pw / 2, py + 48);
      c.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
      c.textAlign = "left";
      c.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    /** 전환: 가운데에서 mid 를 부르고 끝나면 풀린다 */
    /** open: 이미 덮인 상태에서 시작해 열리기만 한다 (상태가 먼저 바뀐 뒤 부를 때) */
    transition(kind, opts = {}) {
      return new Promise((done2) => {
        if (this.tr) {
          const old = this.tr;
          if (!old.midDone) old.mid?.();
          old.done();
        }
        const dur = opts.dur ?? (kind === "doors" ? 1.6 : kind === "glitch" ? 0.7 : 0.8);
        this.tr = { kind, t: opts.open ? dur / 2 : 0, dur, mid: opts.mid, midDone: !!opts.open, x: opts.x ?? W3 / 2, y: opts.y ?? H3 / 2, done: done2, text: opts.text ?? "" };
      });
    }
    /** 맨 마지막(HUD·메뉴 위)에 그린다 */
    drawTransition(c) {
      size(c);
      const tr = this.tr;
      if (!tr) return;
      const half = tr.dur / 2;
      const k = tr.t < half ? easeInOut(tr.t / half) : 1 - easeInOut((tr.t - half) / half);
      c.save();
      c.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      if (tr.kind === "fade") {
        c.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${k})`;
        c.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
      } else if (tr.kind === "doors") {
        const hold = Math.min(1, k * 1.15);
        const dw = W3 / 2 * hold;
        const g = c.createLinearGradient(0, 0, dw, 0);
        g.addColorStop(0, "#3c4448");
        g.addColorStop(1, "#5f696e");
        c.fillStyle = g;
        c.fillRect(0, 0, dw, H3);
        const g2 = c.createLinearGradient(W3, 0, W3 - dw, 0);
        g2.addColorStop(0, "#3c4448");
        g2.addColorStop(1, "#5f696e");
        c.fillStyle = g2;
        c.fillRect(W3 - dw, 0, dw, H3);
        c.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.5)";
        for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) c.fillRect(dw * i / 4, 0, 1, H3), c.fillRect(W3 - dw * i / 4, 0, 1, H3);
        c.fillStyle = "#111";
        c.fillRect(dw - 2, 0, 2, H3);
        c.fillRect(W3 - dw, 0, 2, H3);
        if (hold >= 1 && tr.text) {
          c.fillStyle = "#120604";
          c.fillRect(W3 / 2 - 90, 70, 180, 70);
          setPixelText(true);
          label(c, tr.text, W3 / 2, 124, 44, "#ff8a40", "center", 900);
          setPixelText(false);
        }
      } else if (tr.kind === "iris") {
        const r = Math.hypot(W3, H3) * (1 - k);
        c.fillStyle = "#000";
        c.beginPath();
        c.rect(0, 0, W3, H3);
        c.arc(tr.x, tr.y, Math.max(0, r), 0, Math.PI * 2, true);
        c.fill();
      } else {
        const src = c.canvas;
        const n = 14;
        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          if (Math.random() > 0.35 + k * 0.5) continue;
          const y = Math.floor(Math.random() * H3), h = 4 + Math.random() * 40;
          const dx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 160 * k;
          c.drawImage(src, 0, y, W3, h, dx, y, W3, h);
        }
        c.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${k * 0.85})`;
        c.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
        c.fillStyle = `rgba(180,20,20,${k * 0.25})`;
        for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) c.fillRect(Math.random() * W3, Math.random() * H3, 2 + Math.random() * 80, 2);
      }
      c.restore();
    }
    // ── 그리기 도구 (엔진·렌더러가 쓴다) ──
    /** 땅 그림자: 공중에 뜰수록 작고 옅게 */
    static shadow(c, x, y, w, z = 0) {
      const k = Math.max(0.35, 1 - z / 320);
      c.save();
      c.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${0.45 * k})`;
      c.beginPath();
      c.ellipse(x, y, w / 2 * k, w / 7 * k, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      c.fill();
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 어두운 층: 빛 자리만 뚫린 어둠. 빛은 월드 좌표, camX 를 빼서 그린다 */
    darkness(c, lights, alpha, camX, camY = 0) {
      size(c);
      const d3 = this.dark;
      fit(d3, W3, H3);
      d3.fillStyle = `rgba(2,3,6,${alpha})`;
      d3.fillRect(0, 0, W3, H3);
      d3.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
      for (const l of lights) {
        const r = l.r * (l.flicker ? 0.94 + Math.sin(this.time * 23) * 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.03 : 1);
        const x = l.x - camX, y = l.y - camY;
        const g = d3.createRadialGradient(x, y, r * 0.15, x, y, r);
        g.addColorStop(0, "rgba(0,0,0,1)");
        g.addColorStop(0.55, "rgba(0,0,0,0.75)");
        g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
        d3.fillStyle = g;
        d3.fillRect(x - r, y - r, r * 2, r * 2);
      }
      d3.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
      c.save();
      c.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      c.drawImage(d3.canvas, 0, 0, W3, H3, 0, 0, W3, H3);
      c.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
      for (const l of lights) {
        if (!l.color) continue;
        const x = l.x - camX, y = l.y - camY;
        const g = c.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, l.r);
        g.addColorStop(0, l.color);
        g.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0,0,0,0)");
        c.fillStyle = g;
        c.fillRect(x - l.r, y - l.r, l.r * 2, l.r * 2);
      }
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 실루엣을 한 색으로 (피격 하얀 번쩍). draw 는 (0,0)~(w,h) 안에 그림을 그린다 */
    tint(c, x, y, w, h, draw, color, a = 1) {
      const m = this.m1;
      fit(m, w, h);
      draw(m);
      m.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
      m.fillStyle = color;
      m.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      c.save();
      c.globalAlpha *= a;
      c.drawImage(m.canvas, 0, 0, w, h, x, y, w, h);
      c.restore();
    }
    /** 림라이트: 빛을 향한 가장자리만 밝게. 어두운 층에서 캐릭터가 배경과 갈라져 보이게 한다 */
    rim(c, x, y, w, h, draw, lx, ly, color = "#ffd9a0", a = 0.9, thick = 3) {
      const pad = thick + 1;
      const ww = w + pad * 2, hh = h + pad * 2;
      const s = this.m2, r = this.m1;
      fit(s, ww, hh);
      s.translate(pad, pad);
      draw(s);
      s.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      fit(r, ww, hh);
      r.drawImage(s.canvas, 0, 0, ww, hh, 0, 0, ww, hh);
      r.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
      r.fillStyle = color;
      r.fillRect(0, 0, ww, hh);
      const cx = x + w / 2, cy = y + h / 2;
      const d3 = Math.hypot(lx - cx, ly - cy) || 1;
      const dx = (lx - cx) / d3 * thick, dy = (ly - cy) / d3 * thick;
      r.globalCompositeOperation = "destination-out";
      r.drawImage(s.canvas, 0, 0, ww, hh, -dx, -dy, ww, hh);
      c.save();
      c.globalAlpha *= a;
      c.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
      c.drawImage(r.canvas, 0, 0, ww, hh, x - pad, y - pad, ww, hh);
      c.restore();
    }
  };
  var easeOut = (k) => 1 - (1 - k) * (1 - k);
  var easeInOut = (k) => k < 0.5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2;

  // src/game/action/ui/gamepad.ts
  var DEAD = 0.3;
  var PadReader = class {
    constructor(hub) {
      this.hub = hub;
    }
    connected = "";
    prev = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    rumbleOn = true;
    index = null;
    setRumble(on) {
      this.rumbleOn = on;
    }
    pad() {
      if (typeof navigator === "undefined" || !navigator.getGamepads) return null;
      for (const p of navigator.getGamepads()) if (p && p.connected) return p;
      return null;
    }
    poll() {
      const p = this.pad();
      if (!p) {
        this.connected = "";
        this.index = null;
        this.hub.releaseSource("pad");
        this.prev.clear();
        return;
      }
      const idx = p.index ?? 0;
      if (this.index !== idx || this.connected !== p.id) {
        this.hub.releaseSource("pad");
        this.prev.clear();
      }
      this.index = idx;
      this.connected = p.id;
      const want = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
      const set = (code, on) => want.set(code, (want.get(code) ?? false) || on);
      p.buttons.forEach((b, i) => {
        const code = PAD_BUTTON[i];
        if (code) set(code, b.pressed || b.value > 0.5);
      });
      let x = p.axes[0] ?? 0, y = p.axes[1] ?? 0;
      if (Math.hypot(x, y) < DEAD) x = y = 0;
      this.hub.setAnalog("pad", x, y);
      set("ArrowLeft", x < -0.45);
      set("ArrowRight", x > 0.45);
      set("ArrowUp", y < -0.45);
      set("ArrowDown", y > 0.45);
      for (const code of this.prev.keys()) if (!want.has(code)) want.set(code, false);
      for (const [code, on] of want) {
        if (on && !this.prev.get(code)) this.hub.press(code, "pad");
        else if (!on && this.prev.get(code)) this.hub.release(code, "pad");
        this.prev.set(code, on);
      }
    }
    /** 진동 0~1, ms */
    rumble(strength, ms) {
      if (!this.rumbleOn) return;
      const p = this.pad();
      p?.vibrationActuator?.playEffect?.("dual-rumble", { duration: ms, strongMagnitude: strength, weakMagnitude: Math.min(1, strength * 1.4) })?.catch?.(() => {
      });
    }
  };

  // src/game/action/ui/input.ts
  var MARK = "__goeiSynth";
  var InputHub = class {
    bySrc = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map([
      ["kb", /* @__PURE__ */ new Set()],
      ["touch", /* @__PURE__ */ new Set()],
      ["pad", /* @__PURE__ */ new Set()]
    ]);
    down = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    edgeDown = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    edgeUp = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    since = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    analog = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
    /** 마지막으로 쓴 입력 장치 (안내 글자 고르기용) */
    last = "kb";
    /** 켜면 장치별 입력을 합친 KeyboardEvent만 target에 보낸다 (키보드 원본은 차단). */
    synth = false;
    target = null;
    /** 메뉴가 열려 있으면 게임 입력을 막고 메뉴가 받는다 */
    capture = null;
    listeners = [];
    detach = null;
    now = () => typeof performance !== "undefined" ? performance.now() : Date.now();
    on(fn2) {
      this.listeners.push(fn2);
      return () => this.listeners = this.listeners.filter((f) => f !== fn2);
    }
    press(code, src) {
      code = ALIAS[code] ?? code;
      this.last = src;
      if (this.capture?.(code, true, src)) return;
      const set = this.bySrc.get(src);
      if (set.has(code)) return;
      set.add(code);
      if (!this.down.has(code)) {
        this.down.add(code);
        this.edgeDown.add(code);
        this.since.set(code, this.now());
        this.emit(code, true);
        for (const f of this.listeners) f(code, true, src);
      }
    }
    release(code, src) {
      code = ALIAS[code] ?? code;
      const set = this.bySrc.get(src);
      if (!set.delete(code)) {
        this.capture?.(code, false, src);
        return;
      }
      for (const s of this.bySrc.values()) if (s.has(code)) return;
      this.down.delete(code);
      this.edgeUp.add(code);
      this.emit(code, false);
      for (const f of this.listeners) f(code, false, src);
    }
    releaseSource(src) {
      for (const c of [...this.bySrc.get(src)]) this.release(c, src);
      this.analog.delete(src);
    }
    releaseAll() {
      for (const s of ["kb", "touch", "pad"]) this.releaseSource(s);
    }
    held(code) {
      return this.down.has(code);
    }
    /** 누르고 있던 시간(초). 안 누르면 0 */
    heldFor(code) {
      return this.down.has(code) ? (this.now() - (this.since.get(code) ?? this.now())) / 1e3 : 0;
    }
    pressed(code) {
      return this.edgeDown.has(code);
    }
    released(code) {
      return this.edgeUp.has(code);
    }
    /** 매 프레임(엔진 틱) 끝에 부른다 */
    endFrame() {
      this.edgeDown.clear();
      this.edgeUp.clear();
    }
    setAnalog(src, x, y) {
      if (x === 0 && y === 0) this.analog.delete(src);
      else this.analog.set(src, { x, y });
    }
    /** 이동 방향 -1~1. 아날로그(스틱)가 있으면 그 값, 없으면 방향키 */
    axis() {
      let x = 0, y = 0;
      for (const a of this.analog.values()) x += a.x, y += a.y;
      if (x === 0 && y === 0) {
        x = (this.held("ArrowRight") ? 1 : 0) - (this.held("ArrowLeft") ? 1 : 0);
        y = (this.held("ArrowDown") ? 1 : 0) - (this.held("ArrowUp") ? 1 : 0);
      }
      return { x: Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, x)), y: Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, y)) };
    }
    emit(code, down2) {
      if (!this.synth || !this.target || typeof KeyboardEvent === "undefined") return;
      const ev = new KeyboardEvent(down2 ? "keydown" : "keyup", { code, key: KEY_OF[code] ?? code, bubbles: true, cancelable: true });
      ev[MARK] = true;
      this.target.dispatchEvent(ev);
    }
    /** 키보드 받기. 글 입력칸에 초점이 있으면 무시한다 */
    attachKeyboard(el, opts = {}) {
      this.detach?.();
      const physical = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
      const onKey = (e) => {
        const ev = e;
        if (ev[MARK]) return;
        const t = ev.target;
        const editing = t && (t.tagName === "INPUT" || t.tagName === "TEXTAREA" || t.tagName === "SELECT" || t.isContentEditable);
        if (editing && ev.type === "keydown") return;
        if (this.synth) ev.stopImmediatePropagation();
        if (ev.type === "keydown") {
          if (opts.prevent?.(ev.code) ?? true) ev.preventDefault();
          if (ev.repeat) return;
          physical.add(ev.code);
          this.press(ev.code, "kb");
        } else {
          physical.delete(ev.code);
          const code = ALIAS[ev.code] ?? ev.code;
          if (![...physical].some((c) => (ALIAS[c] ?? c) === code)) this.release(code, "kb");
        }
      };
      const blur = () => {
        physical.clear();
        this.releaseAll();
      };
      const vis = () => document.hidden && blur();
      el.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      el.addEventListener("keyup", onKey);
      window.addEventListener("blur", blur);
      document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", vis);
      this.detach = () => {
        el.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        el.removeEventListener("keyup", onKey);
        window.removeEventListener("blur", blur);
        document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", vis);
      };
    }
    dispose() {
      this.detach?.();
      this.releaseAll();
    }
  };

  // src/game/action/ui/menu.ts
  var esc = (s) => s.replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c]);
  var focusable = (it) => it.type !== "head" && it.type !== "html" && !(it.type === "button" && it.disabled);
  var Menu = class {
    constructor(layer) {
      this.layer = layer;
      this.el = document.createElement("div");
      this.el.className = "mn";
      layer.append(this.el);
      this.el.addEventListener("click", (e) => this.click(e));
      this.el.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
        const row = e.target.closest("[data-i]");
        if (row && e.pointerType === "mouse") this.setFocus(Number(row.dataset.i), false);
      });
    }
    stack = [];
    focus = 0;
    el;
    onChange = null;
    /** 메뉴가 열리고 닫힐 때 (터치 버튼 숨기기 등) */
    onOpen = null;
    /** 소리 스레드가 메뉴 이동·결정 소리를 붙일 자리 */
    onSound = null;
    get isOpen() {
      return this.stack.length > 0;
    }
    get top() {
      return this.stack[this.stack.length - 1] ?? null;
    }
    open(s) {
      this.stack.push(s);
      this.focus = this.first();
      this.render();
    }
    replace(s) {
      this.stack.pop();
      this.open(s);
    }
    back() {
      const s = this.top;
      if (!s || s.noBack) return;
      this.onSound?.("back");
      if (s.back) s.back();
      else this.pop();
    }
    pop() {
      this.stack.pop();
      this.focus = this.first();
      this.render();
    }
    closeAll() {
      this.stack = [];
      this.render();
    }
    /** 화면 내용이 바뀌면 다시 그린다 (초점 유지) */
    refresh() {
      this.render();
    }
    first() {
      const s = this.top;
      if (!s) return 0;
      const i = s.items.findIndex(focusable);
      return Math.max(0, i);
    }
    setFocus(i, sound = true) {
      if (i === this.focus) return;
      this.focus = i;
      if (sound) this.onSound?.("move");
      this.el.querySelectorAll("[data-i]").forEach((r) => r.classList.toggle("f", Number(r.dataset.i) === i));
      this.el.querySelector(`[data-i="${i}"]`)?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
    }
    moveFocus(d3) {
      const s = this.top;
      if (!s) return;
      const n = s.items.length;
      let i = this.focus;
      for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
        i = (i + d3 + n) % n;
        if (focusable(s.items[i])) return this.setFocus(i);
      }
    }
    /** 키 하나를 메뉴가 먹으면 true */
    key(code) {
      const s = this.top;
      if (!s) return false;
      const it = s.items[this.focus];
      switch (code) {
        case "ArrowUp":
          this.moveFocus(-1);
          return true;
        case "ArrowDown":
          this.moveFocus(1);
          return true;
        case "ArrowLeft":
        case "ArrowRight":
          if (it) this.adjust(it, code === "ArrowLeft" ? -1 : 1);
          return true;
        case "Enter":
        case "Space":
        case "NumpadEnter":
          if (it) this.activate(it);
          return true;
        case "Escape":
        case "Backspace":
          this.back();
          return true;
      }
      if (s.numbered && /^Digit[1-9]$/.test(code)) {
        const n = Number(code.slice(5)) - 1;
        const btns = s.items.map((x, i) => [x, i]).filter(([x]) => x.type === "button");
        const pick3 = btns[n];
        if (pick3) this.focus = pick3[1], this.activate(pick3[0]);
        return true;
      }
      return true;
    }
    adjust(it, d3) {
      if (it.type === "slider") {
        const v = Math.max(it.min, Math.min(it.max, Math.round((it.get() + d3 * it.step) / it.step) * it.step));
        it.set(Number(v.toFixed(3)));
      } else if (it.type === "toggle") it.set(!it.get());
      else if (it.type === "choice") {
        const i = it.options.findIndex(([v]) => v === it.get());
        it.set(it.options[(i + d3 + it.options.length) % it.options.length][0]);
      } else return;
      this.onSound?.("move");
      this.render();
      this.onChange?.();
    }
    activate(it) {
      if (it.type === "button") {
        if (it.disabled) return;
        this.onSound?.("ok");
        it.act();
      } else this.adjust(it, 1);
    }
    click(e) {
      const t = e.target;
      const row = t.closest("[data-i]");
      if (t.closest("[data-back]")) return this.back();
      if (!row) return;
      const i = Number(row.dataset.i);
      const it = this.top?.items[i];
      if (!it) return;
      this.setFocus(i, false);
      const step2 = t.closest("[data-step]");
      if (step2) return this.adjust(it, Number(step2.dataset.step));
      if (it.type === "slider") {
        const bar2 = row.querySelector(".mn-bar");
        if (bar2 && t.closest(".mn-bar")) {
          const r = bar2.getBoundingClientRect();
          const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width));
          it.set(Number((it.min + Math.round(k * (it.max - it.min) / it.step) * it.step).toFixed(3)));
          this.render();
          this.onChange?.();
        }
        return;
      }
      const opt = t.closest("[data-opt]");
      if (opt && it.type === "choice") {
        it.set(opt.dataset.opt);
        this.render();
        this.onChange?.();
        return;
      }
      this.activate(it);
    }
    render() {
      const s = this.top;
      this.layer.classList.toggle("menu-on", !!s);
      this.onOpen?.(!!s);
      if (!s) {
        this.el.innerHTML = "";
        this.el.className = "mn";
        return;
      }
      this.el.className = `mn on gk-font mn-${s.look ?? "panel"}`;
      let bn = 0;
      const rows = s.items.map((it, i) => {
        const f = i === this.focus ? " f" : "";
        switch (it.type) {
          case "head":
            return `<div class="mn-head">${esc(it.label)}</div>`;
          case "html":
            return `<div class="mn-html">${it.html}</div>`;
          case "button": {
            bn++;
            const num = s.numbered ? `<em>${bn}</em>` : "";
            return `<button type="button" class="mn-row mn-btn gk-btn${f}${it.danger ? " danger" : ""}" data-i="${i}" ${it.disabled ? "disabled" : ""}>${num}<span>${esc(it.label)}</span>${it.sub ? `<small>${esc(it.sub)}</small>` : ""}</button>`;
          }
          case "toggle":
            return `<div class="mn-row gk-btn${f}" data-i="${i}"><span>${esc(it.label)}</span><b class="mn-tog ${it.get() ? "on" : ""}"><i></i></b></div>`;
          case "slider": {
            const v = it.get();
            const k = (v - it.min) / (it.max - it.min);
            return `<div class="mn-row gk-btn${f}" data-i="${i}"><span>${esc(it.label)}</span><span class="mn-sl"><b data-step="-1">−</b><span class="mn-bar"><i style="width:${(k * 100).toFixed(1)}%"></i></span><b data-step="1">+</b><em>${esc(it.fmt ? it.fmt(v) : String(v))}</em></span></div>`;
          }
          case "choice":
            return `<div class="mn-row gk-btn${f}" data-i="${i}"><span>${esc(it.label)}</span><span class="mn-ch">${it.options.map(([v, l]) => `<b data-opt="${esc(v)}" class="${v === it.get() ? "on" : ""}">${esc(l)}</b>`).join("")}</span></div>`;
        }
      }).join("");
      const back = s.noBack || s.look === "title" ? "" : `<button type="button" class="mn-x" data-back aria-label="뒤로">✕</button>`;
      const title2 = s.look === "title" ? `<div class="mn-logo"><small>관리국 괴이 탐사</small><b>${esc(s.title ?? "")}</b><span>${esc(s.sub ?? "")}</span></div>` : s.title ? `<h2>${esc(s.title)}</h2>${s.sub ? `<p class="mn-sub">${esc(s.sub)}</p>` : ""}` : "";
      this.el.innerHTML = `<div class="mn-box${s.look === "title" ? "" : " gk-panel"}">${back}${title2}<div class="mn-list">${rows}</div></div>`;
      this.el.querySelector(".f")?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
    }
    dispose() {
      this.el.remove();
    }
  };
  function choiceScreen(menu, o, pick3) {
    return {
      id: "choice",
      look: "dialog",
      numbered: true,
      title: o.title,
      noBack: o.cancel === false,
      back: () => pick3(-1),
      items: [
        ...o.lines?.length ? [{ type: "html", html: o.lines.map((l) => `<p>${esc(l)}</p>`).join("") }] : [],
        ...o.options.map((label2, i) => ({ type: "button", label: label2, act: () => pick3(i) }))
      ]
    };
  }

  // src/game/action/ui/settings.ts
  var DEFAULT_SETTINGS2 = {
    crt: "off",
    shake: 1,
    flash: true,
    bright: 1,
    pixel: true,
    charSize: 1,
    master: 0.8,
    music: 0.7,
    sfx: 0.9,
    touch: "auto",
    touchSize: 1,
    touchAlpha: 0.8,
    stickRun: true,
    lefty: false,
    vibrate: true,
    numbers: true,
    talkModel: "main",
    talkRecent: false,
    tScene: 10,
    tRegion: 30,
    tTalk: 10,
    tFight: 10,
    tWaitShort: 30,
    tWaitLong: 60,
    deathMode: "perma",
    npcKill: false
  };
  var KEY = "goei-game-ui:v1";
  var localStore = {
    load() {
      try {
        return localStorage.getItem(KEY);
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    },
    save(text) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(KEY, text);
      } catch {
      }
    }
  };
  function parseSettings(text) {
    const s = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS2 };
    if (!text) return s;
    try {
      const o = JSON.parse(text);
      if (o.crt === true) o.crt = "curved";
      else if (o.crt === false) o.crt = "off";
      for (const k of Object.keys(s)) {
        const v = o[k];
        if (v !== void 0 && typeof v === typeof s[k]) s[k] = v;
      }
    } catch {
    }
    return s;
  }

  var BTN_CELLS = [[10, 6], [135, 6], [260, 6], [385, 6], [10, 132], [135, 132], [260, 132], [385, 132]];
  var BTN_CELL = 118;
  var BTN_INNER = { rx: 48, ry: 46, color: "#292c3b" };

  // src/game/action/ui/touchArt.ts
  var dpr = () => typeof devicePixelRatio === "number" && devicePixelRatio > 0 ? Math.min(4, devicePixelRatio) : 1;
  var RING = {
    main: { hi: "#ffe8a3", mid: "#d9a63c", lo: "#7a4c12", edge: "#3a230a" },
    ult: { hi: "#ffe8a3", mid: "#d9a63c", lo: "#7a4c12", edge: "#3a230a" },

  var sheet = null;
  var sheetReady = false;
  var waiters = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  function loadSheet() {
    if (sheet || typeof Image === "undefined") return;
    sheet = new Image();
    sheet.onload = () => {
      sheetReady = true;
      for (const f of waiters) f();
      waiters.clear();
    };
    sheet.src = BTN_SHEET;
  }
  function onSheetReady(f) {
    if (sheetReady) return f();
    waiters.add(f);
    loadSheet();
  }
  var iconCache = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  function iconOf(cell) {
    if (!sheet || !sheetReady) return null;
    const hit2 = iconCache.get(cell);
    if (hit2) return hit2;
    const r = Math.min(BTN_INNER.rx, BTN_INNER.ry) - 2;
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas");
    cv.width = cv.height = r * 2;
    const g = cv.getContext("2d");
    if (!g) return null;
    const [sx, sy] = BTN_CELLS[cell];
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(r, r, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.clip();
    g.drawImage(sheet, sx + BTN_CELL / 2 - r, sy + BTN_CELL / 2 - r, r * 2, r * 2, 0, 0, r * 2, r * 2);

    const [br, bg, bb] = [41, 44, 59];

    }
    g.putImageData(img6, 0, 0);

    if (x1 < 0) return cv;

    const cx = (x0 + x1 + 1) / 2, cy = (y0 + y1 + 1) / 2;

    tight.getContext("2d").drawImage(cv, Math.round(cx - side / 2), Math.round(cy - side / 2), side, side, 0, 0, side, side);
    iconCache.set(cell, tight);
    return tight;
  }

  function renderButton(a) {
    const k = dpr();
    const key = `${a.kind}|${a.size}|${a.cell ?? ""}|${a.text ?? ""}|${a.on ? 1 : 0}|${k}|${sheetReady ? 1 : 0}`;
    const hit2 = cache6.get(key);
    if (hit2) return hit2;
    const S2 = Math.round(a.size * k);
    const cv = document.createElement("canvas");
    cv.width = cv.height = S2;
    const g = cv.getContext("2d");
    const c = S2 / 2;
    const main = a.kind === "main";
    const ring = RING[a.kind];
    const pad = main ? S2 * 0.085 : S2 * 0.045;
    const R = c - pad;
    const rw = main ? R * 0.17 : R * 0.12;
    g.clearRect(0, 0, S2, S2);
    g.save();
    g.shadowColor = "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)";
    g.shadowBlur = S2 * 0.06;
    g.shadowOffsetY = S2 * 0.02;
    g.fillStyle = ring.edge;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
    g.restore();
    if (main) {
      g.fillStyle = ring.mid;
      g.strokeStyle = ring.edge;
      g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, S2 * 6e-3);
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        const t = i / 8 * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
        const len = i % 2 === 0 ? S2 * 0.085 : S2 * 0.05;
        const w = S2 * 0.035;
        g.save();
        g.translate(c + Math.cos(t) * (R - rw * 0.3), c + Math.sin(t) * (R - rw * 0.3));
        g.rotate(t);
        g.beginPath();
        g.moveTo(0, -w);
        g.lineTo(len + rw * 0.3, 0);
        g.lineTo(0, w);
        g.closePath();
        g.fill();
        g.stroke();
        g.restore();
      }
    }
    const lg = g.createLinearGradient(c - R, c - R, c + R, c + R);
    lg.addColorStop(0, ring.hi);
    lg.addColorStop(0.35, ring.mid);
    lg.addColorStop(0.65, ring.lo);
    lg.addColorStop(1, ring.mid);
    g.fillStyle = lg;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.arc(c, c, R - rw, 0, Math.PI * 2, true);
    g.fill();
    g.strokeStyle = ring.edge;
    g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, S2 * 8e-3);
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.stroke();
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R - rw, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.stroke();
    g.strokeStyle = "rgba(255,255,255,0.45)";
    g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, S2 * 6e-3);
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R - rw * 0.35, Math.PI * 1.05, Math.PI * 1.75);
    g.stroke();
    if (main || a.kind === "ult") {
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const t = i / 4 * Math.PI * 2 + Math.PI / 4;
        const x = c + Math.cos(t) * (R - rw / 2), y = c + Math.sin(t) * (R - rw / 2);
        const rg = g.createRadialGradient(x - rw * 0.1, y - rw * 0.1, 0, x, y, rw * 0.28);
        rg.addColorStop(0, ring.hi);
        rg.addColorStop(1, ring.lo);
        g.fillStyle = rg;
        g.beginPath();
        g.arc(x, y, rw * 0.28, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        g.fill();
      }
    }
    const ir = R - rw;
    const inner = g.createRadialGradient(c, c - ir * 0.35, ir * 0.1, c, c, ir);
    if (a.kind === "auto" && a.on) {
      inner.addColorStop(0, "#7df5e0");
      inner.addColorStop(0.6, "#1f8f7d");
      inner.addColorStop(1, "#0c3d36");
    } else {
      inner.addColorStop(0, "#3a4252");
      inner.addColorStop(0.55, "#171c25");
      inner.addColorStop(1, "#070a0f");
    }
    g.fillStyle = inner;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, ir, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
    const rim = g.createRadialGradient(c, c + ir * 0.9, ir * 0.2, c, c + ir * 0.5, ir * 1.1);
    rim.addColorStop(0, "rgba(120,150,190,0.22)");
    rim.addColorStop(1, "rgba(120,150,190,0)");
    g.fillStyle = rim;
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, ir, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
    const icon2 = a.cell !== null && a.cell !== void 0 ? iconOf(a.cell) : null;
    if (icon2) {
      const avail = ir * 2 * 0.72;
      let s = avail / icon2.width;
      const fl = Math.floor(s);
      if (fl >= 1 && fl / s >= 0.85) s = fl;
      const w = icon2.width * s;
      g.save();
      g.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      g.shadowColor = "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)";
      g.shadowBlur = S2 * 0.02;
      g.shadowOffsetY = Math.max(1, S2 * 0.012);
      g.drawImage(icon2, Math.round(c - w / 2), Math.round(c - w / 2), w, w);
      g.restore();
    } else if (a.kind === "menu") {
      g.fillStyle = "#f2ead6";
      const bw = ir * 0.9, bh = Math.max(2, ir * 0.16);
      for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) g.fillRect(c - bw / 2, c + i * ir * 0.42 - bh / 2, bw, bh);
    } else if (a.text) {
      const lines = a.text.split("\n");
      const longest = Math.max(...lines.map((l) => l.length));
      const want = Math.min(ir * 0.62, ir * 1.7 / Math.max(2, longest), ir * 1.6 / Math.max(2, lines.length));
      const up = Math.ceil(want / 11) * 11 <= want * 1.15;
      const fs = 11 * Math.max(1, up ? Math.ceil(want / 11) : Math.floor(want / 11));
      g.font = `700 ${fs}px "${PIXEL_FONT}", monospace`;
      g.textAlign = "center";
      g.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
      const sh = Math.max(1, Math.round(fs / 11));
      const ms = lines.map((ln) => g.measureText(ln));
      const asc = Math.max(...ms.map((m) => m.actualBoundingBoxAscent || fs * 0.8));
      const desc = Math.max(...ms.map((m) => m.actualBoundingBoxDescent || 0));
      const gap = Math.round(fs * 0.2);
      const lh = asc + desc + gap;
      const block = lines.length * (asc + desc) + (lines.length - 1) * gap + sh;
      const top = c - block / 2;
      lines.forEach((ln, i) => {
        const m = ms[i];
        const off2 = ((m.actualBoundingBoxRight ?? 0) - (m.actualBoundingBoxLeft ?? 0)) / 2;
        const x = Math.round(c - sh / 2 - off2);
        const y = Math.round(top + i * lh + asc);
        g.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.85)";
        g.fillText(ln, x + sh, y + sh);
        g.fillStyle = a.kind === "auto" && a.on ? "#06201c" : "#fbecc9";
        g.fillText(ln, x, y);
      });
    }
    g.save();
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, ir, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.clip();
    const gl = g.createLinearGradient(0, c - ir, 0, c);
    gl.addColorStop(0, "rgba(255,255,255,0.38)");
    gl.addColorStop(1, "rgba(255,255,255,0.0)");
    g.fillStyle = gl;
    g.beginPath();
    g.ellipse(c, c - ir * 0.45, ir * 0.82, ir * 0.5, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.fill();
    g.restore();
    cache6.set(key, cv);
    return cv;
  }
  function renderStickBase(size2) {
    const k = dpr();

    const hit2 = cache6.get(key);
    if (hit2) return hit2;

    g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, S2 * 6e-3);
    g.beginPath();
    g.arc(c, c, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    g.stroke();
    g.fillStyle = "rgba(236,230,212,0.85)";
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const t = i / 4 * Math.PI * 2;
      g.save();
      g.translate(c + Math.cos(t) * (R - rw * 2.2), c + Math.sin(t) * (R - rw * 2.2));
      g.rotate(t);
      g.beginPath();
      g.moveTo(rw * 0.9, 0);
      g.lineTo(-rw * 0.3, -rw * 0.7);
      g.lineTo(-rw * 0.3, rw * 0.7);
      g.closePath();
      g.fill();
      g.restore();
    }
    g.strokeStyle = "rgba(236,230,212,0.25)";
    g.lineWidth = Math.max(1, S2 * 5e-3);
    g.beginPath();
    g.moveTo(c - R * 0.5, c);
    g.lineTo(c + R * 0.5, c);
    g.moveTo(c, c - R * 0.5);
    g.lineTo(c, c + R * 0.5);
    g.stroke();
    cache6.set(key, cv);
    return cv;
  }

  function safeInsets(host) {
    const probe = document.createElement("div");
    probe.style.cssText = "position:fixed;left:0;top:0;width:0;height:0;visibility:hidden;pointer-events:none;padding:env(safe-area-inset-top) env(safe-area-inset-right) env(safe-area-inset-bottom) env(safe-area-inset-left)";
    host.append(probe);
    const cs = getComputedStyle(probe);
    const v = { t: parseFloat(cs.paddingTop) || 0, r: parseFloat(cs.paddingRight) || 0, b: parseFloat(cs.paddingBottom) || 0, l: parseFloat(cs.paddingLeft) || 0 };
    probe.remove();
    return v;
  }

      });
      layer.append(this.root);
      const opt = { passive: false };
      const s = this.surface;
      s.addEventListener("pointerdown", this.onDown, opt);
      s.addEventListener("pointermove", this.onMove, opt);
      s.addEventListener("pointerup", this.onUp, opt);
      s.addEventListener("pointercancel", this.onUp, opt);
      s.addEventListener("lostpointercapture", this.onUp, opt);
      s.addEventListener("contextmenu", this.onContext);
      s.addEventListener("gesturestart", this.onGesture, opt);
      s.addEventListener("dblclick", this.onContext);
      s.addEventListener("touchend", this.onTouchEnd, opt);
      window.addEventListener("pointerup", this.onUp);
      window.addEventListener("pointercancel", this.onUp);
      window.addEventListener("blur", this.onBlur);
      document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", this.onVisibility);
      window.addEventListener("touchstart", this.onAnyTouch, { passive: true, capture: true });
      document.addEventListener("focusin", this.onIme);
      document.addEventListener("focusout", this.onIme);
      if (typeof visualViewport !== "undefined") visualViewport?.addEventListener("resize", this.onIme);

    onContext = (e) => {

      if (this.active()) e.preventDefault();
    };
    onTouchEnd = (e) => {
      if (!this.shown) return;
      const t = e.target;
      if (t && (t.closest(".tp") || !t.closest(EXEMPT)) && e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
    };
    onBlur = () => this.releaseAll();
    onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) this.releaseAll();
    };
    onAnyTouch = () => {
      if (!this.seenTouch) {
        this.seenTouch = true;
        if (!this.shown) this.refresh();

    };
    /** 층이 화면에 90도 돌아 그려지는가 (플러그인 창 회전·껍데기 돌려 보기) */
    rotated() {
      const r = this.layer.getBoundingClientRect();
      const w = this.layer.clientWidth, h = this.layer.clientHeight;
      return Math.abs(w - h) > 4 && Math.abs(r.width - h) < 2 && Math.abs(r.height - w) < 2;
    }
    /** 설정·화면 크기가 바뀌면 다시 배치 */
    layout() {
      this.releaseAll();
      const s = this.st();
      const bw = this.layer.clientWidth || 800, bh = this.layer.clientHeight || 400;
      const sa = typeof getComputedStyle === "undefined" ? { t: 0, r: 0, b: 0, l: 0 } : safeInsets(this.layer);
      const ins = this.rotated() ? { t: sa.r, r: sa.b, b: sa.l, l: sa.t } : sa;
      const portrait = bh > bw * 1.05;
      const u = Math.max(44, Math.min(86, Math.min(bw, bh) * 0.16)) * s.touchSize;
      const dprNow = typeof devicePixelRatio === "number" ? devicePixelRatio : 1;
      if (dprNow !== this.lastDpr) this.lastDpr = dprNow, clearArtCache();
      this.root.style.setProperty("--tp-u", `${u}px`);
      this.root.style.setProperty("--tp-a", String(s.touchAlpha));
      const flip = (x) => s.lefty ? bw - x : x;
      const ax = bw - ins.r - u * 1.275, ay = bh - ins.b - u * 1.275;
      const cornerBottom = bh / 720 * 112 + 6;
      const topNeed = 2.66 * u + 0.4 * u;
      const ky = Math.max(0.72, Math.min(1, (ay - cornerBottom) / topNeed));
      const place = (b, cx, cy) => {
        const r = b.d * u / 2;
        Object.assign(b, { cx, cy, r });
        const el = b.el;
        el.style.width = el.style.height = `${r * 2}px`;
        el.style.left = `${cx - r}px`;
        el.style.top = `${cy - r}px`;
        this.paint(b);
      };
      const autoR = 0.4 * u, rowY = portrait ? ins.t + 10 + autoR : bh - ins.b - 12 - autoR;
      for (const b of this.btns) {
        if (b.id === "auto") place(b, flip(bw / 2 - 0.92 * u), rowY);
        else if (b.id === "menu") place(b, flip(bw / 2), rowY);
        else if (b.id === "char") place(b, flip(bw / 2 + 0.92 * u), rowY);
        else place(b, flip(ax + b.dx * u), ay + b.dy * u * ky);
      }
      const auto = this.btns.find((b) => b.id === "auto");
      const menu = this.btns.find((b) => b.id === "menu");
      const chr = this.btns.find((b) => b.id === "char");
      const nl = Math.min(auto.cx - auto.r, menu.cx - menu.r, chr.cx - chr.r) - 10, nr = Math.max(auto.cx + auto.r, menu.cx + menu.r, chr.cx + chr.r) + 10;
      this.noStick = { x: nl, y: rowY - autoR - 10, w: nr - nl, h: autoR * 2 + 20 };
      const zt = portrait ? Math.max(bh * 0.4, rowY + autoR + 10) : bh * 0.18;
      const zw = bw * 0.5;
      this.zr = { x: s.lefty ? bw - zw : 0, y: zt, w: zw, h: bh - zt };
      Object.assign(this.zone.style, { left: `${this.zr.x}px`, top: `${zt}px`, width: `${zw}px`, height: `${bh - zt}px` });
      this.sr = u * 1.05;
      this.root.style.setProperty("--tp-r", `${this.sr}px`);
      this.paintStick();
      this.restX = ins.l + 24 + this.sr * 1.1;
      this.restY = ins.b + 24 + this.sr * 1.1;
      this.root.classList.toggle("tp-dialog", this.mode === "dialog");
      this.root.classList.toggle("tp-auto-on", this.autoOn);
      for (const b of this.btns) b.el.style.display = this.usable(b) ? "" : "none";
      this.restBase();
    }
    restBase() {
      const s = this.st();
      const zr = this.zr;
      const x = s.lefty ? zr.w - this.restX : this.restX;
      const y = zr.h - this.restY;
      this.placeBase(zr.x + x, zr.y + y);
      this.knob.style.transform = "";
      this.base.classList.remove("on");
    }
    placeBase(x, y) {
      this.base.style.transform = `translate(${x - this.sr}px, ${y - this.sr}px)`;
      this.baseX = x;
      this.baseY = y;
    }
    /** 이 모드에서 보이는 버튼인가 */
    usable(b) {
      if (this.pc) return PC_BAR.has(b.id);
      if (this.mode === "dialog") return !!b.dialog;
      if (this.root.closest(".exploration-map")) return b.id === "menu";
      return !!b.play;
    }
    /** 눌러지는 버튼인가 (흐린 버튼은 자리만 지킨다) */
    pressable(b) {
      return this.usable(b) && !b.el.classList.contains("off");
    }
    /** 옛 호환: 필드 전용 「조사·메뉴만」 규칙은 없앴다 (버튼 종류는 어디서나 고정) */
    setField(_on) {
    }
    /** 놀이 / 대사창 모드 */
    setMode(m) {
      if (this.mode === m) return;
      this.mode = m;
      this.releaseAll();
      this.refresh();
    }
    get touchMode() {
      return this.mode;
    }
    onIme = () => {
      const vv = typeof visualViewport !== "undefined" ? visualViewport : null;
      const a = document.activeElement;
      const typing = /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(a?.tagName ?? "") || !!a?.isContentEditable;
      const shrunk = !!vv && vv.height < innerHeight * 0.75 && typing;
      const up = typing || shrunk;
      if (up === this.imeUp) return;
      this.imeUp = up;
      if (up) this.releaseAll();
      this.refresh();
    };
    get keypad() {
      return this.imeUp;
    }
    refresh() {
      const s = this.st();
      const coarse = typeof matchMedia !== "undefined" && matchMedia("(pointer: coarse)").matches;
      const show = s.touch === "on" || s.touch === "auto" && (coarse || this.seenTouch);
      this.pc = !show && this.visible && !this.imeUp && this.mode === "play";
      this.root.classList.toggle("pc", this.pc);
      this.root.classList.toggle("hide", !show && !this.pc || !this.visible || this.imeUp);
      this.layout();
    }
    setVisible(on) {
      if (this.visible === on) return;
      this.visible = on;
      if (!on) this.releaseAll();
      this.refresh();
    }
    get shown() {
      return !this.root.classList.contains("hide") && !this.pc;
    }
    /** 터치를 받는 중 (보이고 키패드가 아님) */
    active() {
      return (this.shown || this.pc) && !this.imeUp;
    }
    /** 버튼 종류 → 틀 모양 */
    kindOf(b) {
      if (b.id === "light" || b.id === "ok") return "main";
      if (b.id === "ult") return "ult";
      if (b.id === "block" || b.id === "dodge") return "guard";
      if (b.id === "auto" || b.id === "char") return "auto";
      if (b.id === "menu") return "menu";
      return "skill";
    }
    /** 버튼 한 장을 지금 크기·상태로 그린다 (touchArt.ts 캐시) */
    paint(b) {
      const cv = b.el.querySelector(":scope > .tp-cv");
      if (!cv || !b.r) return;
      const size2 = Math.round(b.r * 2);
      const art4 = renderButton({ size: size2, kind: this.kindOf(b), cell: b.cell, text: b.id === "auto" ? this.pc ? "자동" : "자동\n전투" : b.text, on: b.id === "auto" && this.autoOn });
      if (cv.width !== art4.width || cv.height !== art4.height) cv.width = art4.width, cv.height = art4.height;
      const g = cv.getContext("2d");
      if (!g) return;
      g.clearRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
      g.drawImage(art4, 0, 0);
    }
    paintAll() {
      for (const b of this.btns) this.paint(b);
      this.paintStick();
    }
    paintStick() {
      const base2 = this.base.querySelector(":scope > .tp-cv");
      const knob = this.knob.querySelector(".tp-cv");
      if (!base2 || !knob || !this.sr) return;
      const b = renderStickBase(Math.round(this.sr * 2));
      const k = renderKnob(Math.round(this.sr * 0.8));
      for (const [cv, art4] of [[base2, b], [knob, k]]) {
        if (cv.width !== art4.width || cv.height !== art4.height) cv.width = art4.width, cv.height = art4.height;
        cv.getContext("2d")?.drawImage(art4, 0, 0);
      }
    }
    /** 옛 호환: 아이템 칸은 모바일에서 뺐다 */
    setItems(_items) {
    }
    /** 필살 게이지 0~1: 금색 테두리 게이지, 가득 차면 빛남 */
    setGauge(v) {
      const b = this.btns.find((x) => x.id === "ult").el;
      const g = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
      const t = g.toFixed(3);
      if (b.style.getPropertyValue("--g") !== t) b.style.setProperty("--g", t);
      b.classList.toggle("full", g >= 1);
    }
    /** 재사용 대기: frac 0~1 (남은 비율), text = 남은 초. 0 이면 끝 */
    setCool(id, frac, text = "") {
      const b = this.btns.find((x) => x.id === id)?.el;
      if (!b) return;
      const on = frac > 0;
      b.classList.toggle("cool", on);
      b.style.setProperty("--cd", on ? String(Math.min(1, frac)) : "0");
      const i = b.querySelector(".tp-cd");
      if (i.textContent !== text) i.textContent = on ? text : "";
    }
    /** 못 씀: 흐리게, 자리는 그대로 */
    setOff(id, off2) {
      const b = this.btns.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!b || b.el.classList.contains("off") === off2) return;
      b.el.classList.toggle("off", off2);
      if (off2) {
        for (const [pid, st] of this.ptr) if (st.kind === "btn" && st.b === b) this.hold(b, false), st.b = null, void pid;
      }
    }
    /** 자동전투 켜짐 표시 */
    setAuto(on) {
      if (this.autoOn === on) return;
      this.autoOn = on;
      this.root.classList.toggle("tp-auto-on", on);
      const a = this.btns.find((x) => x.id === "auto");
      if (a) this.paint(a);
    }
    /** 버튼 강조 (조사할 것이 있을 때 등) */
    hint(id, on) {
      this.btns.find((x) => x.id === id)?.el.classList.toggle("hint", on);
    }
    /** 시험·화면 확인용: 버튼 자리 (층 좌표) */
    rects() {
      return Object.fromEntries(this.btns.map((b) => [b.id, { x: b.cx, y: b.cy, r: b.r, shown: this.usable(b) && this.shown, off: b.el.classList.contains("off") }]));
    }
    hit(x, y) {
      let best = null, bd = Infinity;
      for (const b of this.btns) {
        if (!this.pressable(b)) continue;
        const d3 = Math.hypot(x - b.cx, y - b.cy) / b.r;
        if (d3 < 1.15 && d3 < bd) best = b, bd = d3;
      }
      return best;
    }
    local(e) {
      if (this.toLocal) return this.toLocal(e.clientX, e.clientY, this.layer);
      const r = this.layer.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
    }
    /** 이 버튼이 누르는 키 */
    codeOf(b) {
      switch (b.id) {
        case "light":
          return CODE.attack;
        case "heavy":
          return CODE.reload;
        case "dodge":
          return CODE.evade;
        case "block":
          return CODE.guard;
        case "jump":
          return CODE.jump;
        case "unique":
          return CODE.unique;
        case "ult":
          return CODE.ult;
        case "interact":
          return CODE.interact;
        case "auto":
          return CODE.auto;
        case "menu":
          return "Escape";
        case "char":
          return "Backquote";
        case "ok":
          return "Enter";
        case "cancel":
          return "Escape";
      }
    }
    key(code, on) {
      const n = (this.count.get(code) ?? 0) + (on ? 1 : -1);
      this.count.set(code, Math.max(0, n));
      if (on && n === 1) this.hub.press(code, "touch");
      else if (!on && n <= 0) this.hub.release(code, "touch");
    }
    later(fn2, ms) {
      const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
        this.timers.delete(t);
        fn2();
      }, ms);
      this.timers.add(t);
      return t;
    }
    hold(b, on) {
      b.el.classList.toggle("on", on);
      if (on && this.st().vibrate) navigator.vibrate?.(8);
      if (b.id === "menu" || b.id === "char") {
        if (on) {
          this.releaseAll();
          this.onMenu(b.id === "menu" ? "Escape" : "Backquote");
        }
        return;
      }
      if (b.id === "dodge") {
        if (on) {
          this.key(CODE.evade, true);
          this.later(() => this.key(CODE.evade, false), DODGE_MS);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (b.id === "unique") {
        const charge2 = TOUCH_CHARGE.code;
        if (!charge2) {
          this.key(CODE.unique, on);
          return;
        }
        const u = this.uniq;
        if (on) {
          if (u.down) return;
          u.down = true;
          u.charging = false;
          u.timer = this.later(() => {
            u.timer = 0;
            if (!u.down) return;
            u.charging = true;
            this.key(charge2, true);
          }, CHARGE_MS);
        } else {
          if (!u.down) return;
          u.down = false;
          if (u.timer) clearTimeout(u.timer), this.timers.delete(u.timer), u.timer = 0;
          if (u.charging) this.key(charge2, false);
          else {
            this.key(CODE.unique, true);
            this.later(() => this.key(CODE.unique, false), DODGE_MS);
          }
          u.charging = false;
        }
        return;
      }
      const code = this.codeOf(b);
      if (code) this.key(code, on);
    }
    down(e) {
      if (!this.active() || e.pointerType === "mouse" && e.button !== 0) return;
      const t = e.target;
      const p = this.local(e);
      if (this.pc) {
        const pb = this.hit(p.x, p.y);
        if (!pb) return;
        e.preventDefault();
        this.ptr.set(e.pointerId, { kind: "btn", b: pb });
        this.hold(pb, true);
        return;
      }
      let b = this.mode === "play" ? this.hit(p.x, p.y) : null;
      if (!b && t && !t.closest(".tp") && t.closest(EXEMPT)) return;
      b ??= this.hit(p.x, p.y);
      e.preventDefault();
      try {
        this.surface.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      } catch {
      }
      if (b) {
        this.ptr.set(e.pointerId, { kind: "btn", b });
        this.hold(b, true);
        return;
      }
      const zr = this.zr, ns = this.noStick;
      const inZone = p.x >= zr.x && p.y >= zr.y && p.x <= zr.x + zr.w && p.y <= zr.y + zr.h;
      const blocked = this.mode === "play" && p.x >= ns.x && p.x <= ns.x + ns.w && p.y >= ns.y && p.y <= ns.y + ns.h;
      if (this.stickPid === null && inZone && !blocked && !this.root.closest(".exploration-map")) {
        this.stickPid = e.pointerId;
        this.ptr.set(e.pointerId, { kind: "stick" });
        this.placeBase(p.x, p.y);
        this.base.classList.add("on");
        this.stickTo(p.x, p.y);
        return;
      }
      this.ptr.set(e.pointerId, { kind: "eat" });
    }
    move(e) {
      const st = this.ptr.get(e.pointerId);
      if (!st) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      if (st.kind === "eat") return;
      const p = this.local(e);
      if (st.kind === "stick") {
        this.stickTo(p.x, p.y);
        return;
      }
      const b = this.hit(p.x, p.y);
      if (b !== st.b) {
        if (st.b) this.hold(st.b, false);
        st.b = b;
        if (b) this.hold(b, true);
      }
    }
    up(e) {
      const st = this.ptr.get(e.pointerId);
      if (!st) return;
      this.ptr.delete(e.pointerId);
      try {
        if (this.surface.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) this.surface.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      } catch {
      }
      if (st.kind === "btn") {
        if (st.b) this.hold(st.b, false);
      } else if (st.kind === "stick") {
        this.stickPid = null;
        this.setStick(0, 0);
        this.restBase();
      }
    }
    stickTo(x, y) {
      const R = this.sr * 0.95;
      let dx = x - this.baseX, dy = y - this.baseY;
      const m = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (m > R * 1.5) {
        const k = (m - R * 1.5) / m;
        this.placeBase(this.baseX + dx * k, this.baseY + dy * k);
        dx = x - this.baseX;
        dy = y - this.baseY;
      }
      const mm = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      const cl = mm > R ? R / mm : 1;
      this.knob.style.transform = `translate(${dx * cl}px, ${dy * cl}px)`;
      let nx = dx * cl / R, ny = dy * cl / R;
      if (Math.hypot(nx, ny) < 0.18) nx = ny = 0;
      this.
